```js
// "simplified evaluation function" tables (Tomasz Michniewski). KE is the king in the endgame.
const PST = {
  P: [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 50, 50, 50, 50, 50, 50, 50, 50, 10, 10, 20, 30, 30, 20, 10, 10, 5, 5, 10, 25, 25, 10, 5, 5, 0, 0, 0, 20, 20, 0, 0, 0, 5, -5, -10, 0, 0, -10, -5, 5, 5, 10, 10, -20, -20, 10, 10, 5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0],
  N: [-50, -40, -30, -30, -30, -30, -40, -50, -40, -20, 0, 0, 0, 0, -20, -40, -30, 0, 10, 15, 15, 10, 0, -30, -30, 5, 15, 20, 20, 15, 5, -30, -30, 0, 15, 20, 20, 15, 0, -30, -30, 5, 10, 15, 15, 10, 5, -30, -40, -20, 0, 5, 5, 0, -20, -40, -50, -40, -30, -30, -30, -30, -40, -50],
  B: [-20, -10, -10, -10, -10, -10, -10, -20, -10, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -10, -10, 0, 5, 10, 10, 5, 0, -10, -10, 5, 5, 10, 10, 5, 5, -10, -10, 0, 10, 10, 10, 10, 0, -10, -10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, -10, -10, 5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 5, -10, -20, -10, -10, -10, -10, -10, -10, -20],
  R: [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 5, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 5, -5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -5, -5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -5, -5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -5, -5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -5, -5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -5, 0, 0, 0, 5, 5, 0, 0, 0],
  Q: [-20, -10, -10, -5, -5, -10, -10, -20, -10, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, -10, -10, 0, 5, 5, 5, 5, 0, -10, -5, 0, 5, 5, 5, 5, 0, -5, 0, 0, 5, 5, 5, 5, 0, -5, -10, 5, 5, 5, 5, 5, 0, -10, -10, 0, 5, 0, 0, 0, 0, -10, -20, -10, -10, -5, -5, -10, -10, -20],
  K: [-30, -40, -40, -50, -50, -40, -40, -30, -30, -40, -40, -50, -50, -40, -40, -30, -30, -40, -40, -50, -50, -40, -40, -30, -30, -40, -40, -50, -50, -40, -40, -30, -20, -30, -30, -40, -40, -30, -30, -20, -10, -20, -20, -20, -20, -20, -20, -10, 20, 20, 0, 0, 0, 0, 20, 20, 20, 30, 10, 0, 0, 10, 30, 20],
  KE: [-50, -40, -30, -20, -20, -30, -40, -50, -30, -20, -10, 0, 0, -10, -20, -30, -30, -10, 20, 30, 30, 20, -10, -30, -30, -10, 30, 40, 40, 30, -10, -30, -30, -10, 30, 40, 40, 30, -10, -30, -30, -10, 20, 30, 30, 20, -10, -30, -30, -30, 0, 0, 0, 0, -30, -30, -50, -30, -30, -30, -30, -30, -30, -50],
};
// the score of a position for the side to move, in centipawns (100 = a pawn): material and where the pieces stand
// (the king's table moving from staying sheltered to coming out as the pieces come off), then the pawns (passed pawns
// worth more the further on they are, doubled and isolated pawns worth less), rooks on open files, the bishop pair,
// and the pawns sheltering a castled king while there's still a middlegame
const PASSED_MG = [0, 5, 5, 10, 15, 25, 40, 0], PASSED_EG = [0, 10, 15, 25, 45, 75, 120, 0];
function evaluate(pos) {
  const b = pos.b, wp = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0], bp = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0], wMax = new Array(8).fill(-1), bMin = new Array(8).fill(8);
  const wMin = new Array(8).fill(8), bMax = new Array(8).fill(-1);
  let sc = 0, phase = 0, wk = 4, bk = 60, wb = 0, bb = 0;
  for (let s = 0; s < 64; s++) {
    const p = b[s];
    if (!p) continue;
    const k = kind(p), w = p === k, f = s & 7, r = s >> 3;
    if (k === 'K') { if (w) wk = s; else bk = s; continue; }
    const v = VALUE[k] + PST[k][w ? s ^ 56 : s];
    sc += w ? v : -v; phase += PHASE[k];
    if (k === 'P') { if (w) { wp[f]++; wMax[f] = Math.max(wMax[f], r); wMin[f] = Math.min(wMin[f], r); } else { bp[f]++; bMin[f] = Math.min(bMin[f], r); bMax[f] = Math.max(bMax[f], r); } }
    if (k === 'B') { if (w) wb++; else bb++; }
  }
  const t = Math.min(phase, 24) / 24;
  let x = t * (PST.K[wk ^ 56] - PST.K[bk]) + (1 - t) * (PST.KE[wk ^ 56] - PST.KE[bk]);
  if (wb >= 2) x += 30; if (bb >= 2) x -= 30;
  for (let s = 0; s < 64; s++) {
    const p = b[s];
    if (p !== 'P' && p !== 'p' && p !== 'R' && p !== 'r') continue;
    const f = s & 7, r = s >> 3, lo = Math.max(0, f - 1), hi = Math.min(7, f + 1);
    if (p === 'P') {
      let free = true; for (let g = lo; g <= hi; g++) if (bMax[g] > r) free = false;   // no black pawn ahead on this file or beside it
      if (free) x += t * PASSED_MG[r] + (1 - t) * PASSED_EG[r];
      if ((f === 0 || !wp[f - 1]) && (f === 7 || !wp[f + 1])) x -= 12;
    } else if (p === 'p') {
      let free = true; for (let g = lo; g <= hi; g++) if (wMin[g] < r) free = false;
      if (free) x -= t * PASSED_MG[7 - r] + (1 - t) * PASSED_EG[7 - r];
      if ((f === 0 || !bp[f - 1]) && (f === 7 || !bp[f + 1])) x += 12;
    } else {
      const own = p === 'R' ? wp[f] : bp[f], theirs = p === 'R' ? bp[f] : wp[f], v = !own ? (theirs ? 9 : 18) : 0;
      x += p === 'R' ? v : -v;
    }
  }
  for (let f = 0; f < 8; f++) { if (wp[f] > 1) x -= 12 * (wp[f] - 1); if (bp[f] > 1) x += 12 * (bp[f] - 1); }
  // the king's pawn shelter: own pawns just in front of it and beside, while there's a middlegame to be safe from
  const shelter = (k, pawn, dir) => { let n = 0; for (let df = -1; df <= 1; df++) for (const dr of [1, 2]) { const q = at((k & 7) + df, (k >> 3) + dir * dr); if (q >= 0 && b[q] === pawn) n++; } return Math.min(n, 3); };
  if ((wk >> 3) <= 1) x += 10 * t * shelter(wk, 'P', 1);
  if ((bk >> 3) >= 6) x -= 10 * t * shelter(bk, 'p', -1);
  sc += Math.round(x);
  return pos.turn === 'w' ? sc : -sc;
}
const MATE = 100000;
// captures first, the most valuable piece taken by the least valuable taker; promotions with them
const order = ms => ms.sort((a, b) => ((b.cap ? VALUE[kind(b.cap)] * 10 - VALUE[kind(b.p)] : 0) + (b.promo ? 8000 : 0)) - ((a.cap ? VALUE[kind(a.cap)] * 10 - VALUE[kind(a.p)] : 0) + (a.promo ? 8000 : 0)));
const leaves = (pos, n) => { const k = kingSq(n.b, pos.turn); return k >= 0 && attacked(n.b, k, n.turn); };   // a move that leaves the mover's own king attacked
function quiesce(pos, alpha, beta, ctx) {
  ctx.nodes++;
  const stand = evaluate(pos);
  if (stand >= beta) return stand;
  if (stand > alpha) alpha = stand;
  for (const m of order(pseudoMoves(pos).filter(m => m.cap || m.promo))) {
    const n = makeMove(pos, m);
    if (leaves(pos, n)) continue;
    const sc = -quiesce(n, -beta, -alpha, ctx);
    if (sc >= beta) return sc;
    if (sc > alpha) alpha = sc;
  }
  return alpha;
}
function negamax(pos, depth, alpha, beta, ply, ctx) {
  const chk = inCheck(pos);
  if (chk && ctx.qs && ply < 12) depth++;   // in check: look one move further, so the search never stops with a king attacked
  if (depth <= 0) { if (ctx.qs) return quiesce(pos, alpha, beta, ctx); ctx.nodes++; return evaluate(pos); }
  ctx.nodes++;
  if (pos.half >= 100) return 0;
  let legal = 0, best = -Infinity;
  for (const m of order(pseudoMoves(pos))) {
    const n = makeMove(pos, m);
    if (leaves(pos, n)) continue;
    legal++;
    const sc = -negamax(n, depth - 1, -beta, -alpha, ply + 1, ctx);
    if (sc > best) best = sc;
    if (sc > alpha) alpha = sc;
    if (alpha >= beta) break;
  }
  if (!legal) return chk ? -MATE + ply : 0;
  return best;
}
// a seeded random number generator (mulberry32): the computer's choices repeat exactly for the same seed
function rng(seed) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => { a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0; let t = a; t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1); t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61); return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296; };
}
// Chooses the computer's move. A generator: it yields after scoring each move, so the page can spread the thinking
// over several frames; run it to the end (see runThink) and its return value is the move. cfg: depth (moves looked
// ahead), qs (follow captures to the end), temp (centipawns: how readily it picks a middling move), blunder (the chance,
// each move, of a blunder: see below), and style bonuses in centipawns for the moves the player likes (capture, check,
// pawn, castle, queen).
const BLUNDER = 200;   // a blunder loses at least this much (centipawns): a piece, two pawns, or walking into a mate
const pick = (items, w, rnd) => { let r = rnd() * w.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) { r -= w[i]; if (r <= 0) return items[i]; } return items[items.length - 1]; };
function* think(g, cfg, rnd) {
  const pos = g.pos, moves = legalMoves(pos), ctx = { nodes: 0, qs: !!cfg.qs }, st = cfg.style || {};
  if (!moves.length) return null;
  const slip = rnd() < (cfg.blunder || 0);   // this move, a blunder (if there's one to make)
  // the opening: a little more variety, so the same moves aren't played every game
  const temp = Math.max(1, cfg.temp || 1, g.list.length < 8 ? 30 : 0), scored = [];
  // a move scoring far below the best so far would almost never be picked, so its exact score isn't needed: the search
  // only has to show it's below this floor (much quicker)
  let floor = -Infinity;
  for (const m of order(moves.slice())) {
    const n = makeMove(pos, m), k = repKey(n);
    // a move that repeats the position a third time is a draw; otherwise search the reply
    // (looking one move ahead, the search doesn't see the reply, so a mate in one or a stalemate is checked here)
    const shallow = (cfg.depth || 1) <= 1 && !legalMoves(n).length;
    const raw = g.keys.filter(x => x === k).length >= 2 ? 0 : shallow ? (inCheck(n) ? MATE : 0) : -negamax(n, (cfg.depth || 1) - 1, -Infinity, -floor, 1, ctx);
    floor = Math.max(floor, raw - Math.max(60, 12 * temp));
    let sc = raw;
    if (m.cap) sc += st.capture || 0;
    if (kind(m.p) === 'P') sc += st.pawn || 0;
    if (m.castle) sc += st.castle || 0;
    if (kind(m.p) === 'Q') sc += st.queen || 0;
    if (st.check && inCheck(n)) sc += st.check;
    scored.push([m, sc]);
    yield ctx.nodes;
  }
  const top = Math.max(...scored.map(x => x[1]));
  if (slip) {
    // A blunder, the way people make one: of the moves that lose at least BLUNDER, the one that looks best at a glance,
    // judging only the position straight after it, not the reply (grabbing a pawn the queen can't hold, say). With no
    // such move to make, the move is chosen as usual.
    const bad = scored.filter(([, sc]) => sc <= top - BLUNDER);
    if (bad.length) {
      const glance = bad.map(([m]) => -evaluate(makeMove(pos, m))), hi = Math.max(...glance);
      return pick(bad, glance.map(v => Math.exp(Math.max(-50, (v - hi) / 50))), rnd)[0];
    }
  }
  return pick(scored, scored.map(([, sc]) => Math.exp(Math.max(-50, (sc - top) / temp))), rnd)[0];
}
// runs think() to the end at once (the tests and the tools; the page spreads it over frames instead)
function runThink(g, cfg, rnd) { const it = think(g, cfg, rnd); let r; while (!(r = it.next()).done); return r.value; }

return { START, FILES, sq, sqName, colour, other, kind, shade, parseFen, toFen, attacked, inCheck, pseudoMoves, legalMoves, makeMove, perft,
  san, uci, repKey, deadPosition, judge, newGame, play, playUci, undo, finish, VALUE, evaluate, negamax, rng, think, runThink };
})();
if (typeof module !== 'undefined') module.exports = CORE;
```

## src/bots.js

The computer opponents: names, faces, blurbs, ratings and settings. No DOM; also runs in Node. (49 lines)

```js
// ============================================================================
//  RETRO SET — the computer opponents: who they are, how they look and how they play
// ============================================================================
// No DOM here: this file also runs in Node, for the tests and tools/sim-bots.js. Each bot's rating is the target its
// settings were tuned to, by playing thousands of games: the top two against Stockfish at its calibrated strength
// settings (1500 and 1320), then each of the others 150 points below the bot above it. See HANDOVER.md, section 5,
// for the measured results.
const BOTS = (() => {
'use strict';

// The strength knob, from 0 (the engine's best at that depth) upwards: how readily the bot plays a middling move, as a
// temperature in centipawns (at k 1.4 it's above 1000, close to random). tools/sim-bots.js tunes k.
const knob = k => ({ temp: Math.round(5 + 595 * Math.pow(k, 1.5)) });
// look: the pixel portrait (game.js draws it: sprite style, skin, hair, shirt). cfg: see think() in core.js. depth, qs
// and blunder (the chance, each move, of a real blunder: a move losing a piece or more) are chosen by hand, falling as
// the bots get stronger but never zero; k is found by tools/sim-bots.js. style: bonuses (centipawns) for the moves the
// bot likes.
const LIST = [
  { id: 'torben', name: 'Torben', rating: 100, look: { s: 'cap', skin: '#f6d2b4', hair: '#d9a441', shirt: '#6cb8ff' },
    blurb: 'Has just learnt how the pieces move, and leaves them anywhere.', cfg: { depth: 1, qs: true, blunder: 0.30, k: 0.279 } },
  { id: 'alfie', name: 'Alfie', rating: 250, look: { s: 'man', skin: '#f2c49b', hair: '#b5432a', shirt: '#1d8a74' },
    blurb: 'Loves pushing pawns, and forgets the pieces behind them.', cfg: { depth: 1, qs: true, blunder: 0.24, k: 0.248, style: { pawn: 30 } } },
  { id: 'ollie', name: 'Ollie', rating: 400, look: { s: 'glasses', skin: '#d9a07a', hair: '#1a1206', shirt: '#ff6f8f' },
    blurb: 'Grabs anything you offer, even when it\'s a trap.', cfg: { depth: 1, qs: true, blunder: 0.19, k: 0.237, style: { capture: 80 } } },
  { id: 'dom', name: 'Dom', rating: 550, look: { s: 'beard', skin: '#a8714a', hair: '#1a1206', shirt: '#ffc56b' },
    blurb: 'Gets the queen out early and goes hunting.', cfg: { depth: 1, qs: true, blunder: 0.15, k: 0.157, style: { queen: 25 } } },
  { id: 'joseph', name: 'Joseph', rating: 700, look: { s: 'man', skin: '#6b4429', hair: '#1a1206', shirt: '#56399a' },
    blurb: 'Knows a few openings, then loses the thread.', cfg: { depth: 1, qs: true, blunder: 0.12, k: 0.111 } },
  { id: 'barney', name: 'Barney', rating: 850, look: { s: 'bald', skin: '#f2c49b', hair: '#c9c3bd', shirt: '#b04a7a' },
    blurb: 'A club-night regular. Takes any piece you leave loose.', cfg: { depth: 2, qs: true, blunder: 0.09, k: 0.133 } },
  { id: 'magnus', name: 'Magnus', rating: 1000, look: { s: 'long', skin: '#f6d2b4', hair: '#d9a441', shirt: '#f7ead2' },
    blurb: 'Plays it safe: castles early and waits for you to slip.', cfg: { depth: 2, qs: true, blunder: 0.07, k: 0.076, style: { castle: 40 } } },
  { id: 'jake', name: 'Jake', rating: 1150, look: { s: 'cap', skin: '#d9a07a', hair: '#5a3420', shirt: '#7a8b3a' },
    blurb: 'Steady. Rarely hangs a piece, but sometimes drifts.', cfg: { depth: 2, qs: true, blunder: 0.05, k: 0.062 } },
  { id: 'daws', name: 'Daws', rating: 1300, look: { s: 'glasses', skin: '#f2c49b', hair: '#5a3420', shirt: '#1d8a74' },
    blurb: 'Sharp tactics: looks for checks and captures first.', cfg: { depth: 3, qs: true, blunder: 0.04, k: 0.062, style: { check: 25 } } },
  { id: 'ted', name: 'Ted', rating: 1500, look: { s: 'beard', skin: '#f6d2b4', hair: '#c9c3bd', shirt: '#56399a' },
    blurb: 'The strongest here. Gives very little away.', cfg: { depth: 4, qs: true, blunder: 0.03, k: 0.046 } },
];
// the bots before they were renamed (a saved game or menu choice carries on against the nearest new one)
const RENAMED = { pip: 'torben', mabel: 'alfie', dev: 'dom', priya: 'joseph', gus: 'barney', ruth: 'jake', tariq: 'daws', nell: 'ted' };
// a bot's full settings for think(): its own, with the knob turned into a temperature
const settings = (bot, k = bot.cfg.k) => ({ ...bot.cfg, ...knob(k) });
const byId = id => LIST.find(b => b.id === (RENAMED[id] || id));

return { LIST, knob, settings, byId };
})();
if (typeof module !== 'undefined') module.exports = BOTS;
```

## src/looks.js

The cosmetics: the catalogue of looks, the cases and keys, what a game and a day pay, and the saved locker. No DOM; also runs in Node. (206 lines)

```js
// ============================================================================
//  RETRO SET — the cosmetics: what there is to own, the cases and keys, and the locker
// ============================================================================
// No DOM here: this file also runs in Node, for the tests and tools/sim-shop.js. It knows what each look is called,
// which slot it fills and how rare it is; what a look looks like is drawn elsewhere (sets.js, game.js), by its id.
// Nothing here uses Math.random: every draw takes a generator (CORE.rng), so a test or a measurement repeats exactly.
const LOOKS = (() => {
'use strict';

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ the catalogue
// The seven slots of the locker, in the order the overview shows them.
const SLOTS = [
  { id: 'set', name: 'Pieces' }, { id: 'finish', name: 'Finish' }, { id: 'board', name: 'Board' }, { id: 'room', name: 'Room' },
  { id: 'fx', name: 'Effects' }, { id: 'avatar', name: 'Avatar' }, { id: 'marks', name: 'Square marks' },
];
// Five rarities, as in Retro Rack: the colour, and the coins a duplicate pays (it stays in the locker as well).
const RARITIES = ['common', 'rare', 'epic', 'legendary', 'mythic'];
const RARITY = {
  common: { name: 'Common', colour: '#b9c2cf', dupe: 15 }, rare: { name: 'Rare', colour: '#6cb8ff', dupe: 35 }, epic: { name: 'Epic', colour: '#c58cff', dupe: 80 },
  legendary: { name: 'Legendary', colour: '#ffc56b', dupe: 180 }, mythic: { name: 'Mythic', colour: '#ff6f8f', dupe: 400 },
};
// Every look: [id, name, rarity] by slot; the first of each slot is free (everyone owns it, and a new locker wears it).
// An id is unique within its slot only, so a look's full name is 'slot:id' (key()).
const BY_SLOT = {
  set: [['classic', 'Classic', 'free'], ['minimal', 'Minimal', 'common'], ['carved', 'Carved', 'rare'], ['blocks', 'Blocks', 'rare'], ['ornate', 'Ornate', 'epic'],
    ['scifi', 'Sci-fi', 'legendary'], ['figures', 'Figures', 'mythic']],
  finish: [['classic', 'Classic', 'free'], ['wood', 'Wood', 'common'], ['chalk', 'Chalk', 'common'], ['sea', 'Sea', 'common'], ['forest', 'Forest', 'common'],
    ['marble', 'Marble', 'rare'], ['metal', 'Metal', 'rare'], ['ember', 'Ember', 'rare'], ['candy', 'Candy', 'rare'],
    ['neon', 'Neon', 'epic'], ['ice', 'Ice', 'epic'], ['obsidian', 'Obsidian', 'epic'], ['royal', 'Royal', 'legendary'], ['circuit', 'Circuit', 'legendary'],
    ['rainbow', 'Rainbow', 'mythic'], ['molten', 'Molten', 'mythic']],
  board: [['standard', 'Standard', 'free'], ['tournament', 'Tournament', 'common'], ['walnut', 'Walnut', 'common'], ['slate', 'Slate', 'common'], ['ocean', 'Ocean', 'common'],
    ['rosewood', 'Rosewood', 'rare'], ['mint', 'Mint', 'rare'], ['sand', 'Sand', 'rare'], ['hall', 'Marble hall', 'epic'], ['grid', 'Neon grid', 'epic'],
    ['galaxy', 'Galaxy', 'legendary'], ['lava', 'Lava', 'mythic']],
  room: [['parlour', 'Parlour', 'free'], ['library', 'Library', 'rare'], ['cafe', 'Café', 'rare'], ['park', 'Park', 'epic'], ['arcade', 'Arcade', 'legendary'], ['space', 'Deep space', 'mythic']],
  fx: [['none', 'None', 'free'], ['dust', 'Dust', 'common'], ['sparks', 'Sparks', 'rare'], ['confetti', 'Confetti', 'rare'], ['shockwave', 'Shockwave', 'epic'],
    ['lightning', 'Lightning', 'epic'], ['fireworks', 'Fireworks', 'legendary'], ['blackhole', 'Black hole', 'mythic']],
  avatar: [['plain', 'Player', 'free'], ['curly', 'Curly', 'common'], ['cap', 'Cap', 'common'], ['specs', 'Specs', 'common'], ['beard', 'Beard', 'common'],
    ['beanie', 'Beanie', 'rare'], ['shades', 'Shades', 'rare'], ['headset', 'Headset', 'rare'], ['knight', 'Knight', 'epic'], ['jester', 'Jester', 'epic'],
    ['robot', 'Robot', 'legendary'], ['wizard', 'Wizard', 'mythic']],
  marks: [['standard', 'Standard', 'free'], ['mint', 'Mint', 'common'], ['rose', 'Rose', 'common'], ['mono', 'Mono', 'common'], ['corners', 'Corners', 'rare'],
    ['arrow', 'Arrow', 'rare'], ['pulse', 'Pulse', 'epic'], ['stars', 'Stars', 'epic'], ['flame', 'Flame', 'legendary']],
};
const key = (slot, id) => slot + ':' + id;
const ITEMS = [], ALL = {};
for (const s of SLOTS) for (const [id, name, rarity] of BY_SLOT[s.id]) { const it = { key: key(s.id, id), id, slot: s.id, name, rarity: rarity === 'free' ? 'common' : rarity, free: rarity === 'free' }; ITEMS.push(it); ALL[it.key] = it; }
const FREE = ITEMS.filter(it => it.free).map(it => it.key);
const slotItems = slot => ITEMS.filter(it => it.slot === slot);

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ cases and keys
// A case's grade sets the chance of each rarity (percent: common, rare, epic, legendary, mythic); its price is what the
// shop asks. The Super Lucky Box never gives a duplicate. Every case needs a key: the standard key opens it for any
// slot, a slot's own key guarantees a look for that slot. A key never changes the rarity.
const GRADES = {
  bronze: { name: 'Bronze case', odds: [62, 28, 8, 1.6, 0.4], price: 40 },
  silver: { name: 'Silver case', odds: [38, 38, 18, 5, 1], price: 90 },
  gold: { name: 'Gold case', odds: [14, 36, 32, 14, 4], price: 200 },
  diamond: { name: 'Diamond case', odds: [0, 20, 42, 28, 10], price: 420 },
  lucky: { name: 'Super Lucky Box', odds: [0, 6, 34, 38, 22], price: 1500, fresh: true },
};
const GRADE_IDS = Object.keys(GRADES);
const KEYS = { any: { name: 'Standard key', price: 60 } };
for (const s of SLOTS) KEYS[s.id] = { name: s.name + ' key', price: 110, slot: s.id };
const KEY_IDS = Object.keys(KEYS);
// OWNED_WEIGHT: how likely a look you own is to come up again, against one you don't (1 would be an even draw).
// PITY: an epic or better is certain within this many cases. LUCKY_FULL: a Super Lucky Box with nothing left to give.
const OWNED_WEIGHT = 0.3, PITY = 6, LUCKY_FULL = 1000;

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ earning
// A win against the computer pays coins by the bot's rating and drops WIN_CASES cases (three, since a game of chess
// takes a while: the owner's choice), the grade by how strong the bot is; the first win against each bot brings one
// more case, one grade better (a Super Lucky Box for the three strongest). A draw
// pays a quarter and drops nothing. Opening the game on a new day pays a gift that grows over a week of days running;
// the seventh day in a row brings a Super Lucky Box, and the week starts again.
const winCoins = rating => 60 + Math.round(rating / 5), WIN_CASES = 3;
const gradeFor = rating => rating < 500 ? 'bronze' : rating < 950 ? 'silver' : rating < 1250 ? 'gold' : 'diamond';
const better = g => GRADE_IDS[Math.min(GRADE_IDS.indexOf(g) + 1, GRADE_IDS.indexOf('diamond'))];
const DAILY = [40, 50, 60, 70, 80, 90, 120];
const START = { coins: 250, cases: { silver: 1 }, keys: { any: 1 } };

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ the locker
// { v, coins, owned: { 'slot:id': how many }, cases: { grade: n }, keys: { key: n }, wear: { slot: id }, beaten: [bot
// ids], daily: { last: 'YYYY-MM-DD', streak }, pity, opened }. Every function below takes a locker and changes it in
// place, returning what happened; validate() makes a clean one from anything saved or sent.
const count = (l, k) => l.owned[k] || 0, owns = (l, k) => count(l, k) > 0;
function fresh() {
  const l = { v: 1, coins: START.coins, owned: {}, cases: {}, keys: {}, wear: {}, beaten: [], daily: { last: '', streak: 0 }, pity: 0, opened: 0 };
  for (const g of GRADE_IDS) l.cases[g] = START.cases[g] || 0;
  for (const k of KEY_IDS) l.keys[k] = START.keys[k] || 0;
  for (const k of FREE) { l.owned[k] = 1; l.wear[ALL[k].slot] = ALL[k].id; }
  return l;
}
const whole = (v, max = 1e9) => Number.isFinite(+v) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(max, Math.floor(+v))) : 0;
function validate(d) {
  const l = fresh();
  if (!d || typeof d !== 'object') return l;
  l.coins = whole(d.coins); l.pity = whole(d.pity, PITY); l.opened = whole(d.opened);
  for (const it of ITEMS) { const n = whole(d.owned && d.owned[it.key], 999); if (n || it.free) l.owned[it.key] = Math.max(n, it.free ? 1 : 0); }
  for (const g of GRADE_IDS) l.cases[g] = whole(d.cases && d.cases[g], 9999);
  for (const k of KEY_IDS) l.keys[k] = whole(d.keys && d.keys[k], 9999);
  for (const s of SLOTS) { const id = d.wear && d.wear[s.id]; if (typeof id === 'string' && owns(l, key(s.id, id))) l.wear[s.id] = id; }
  if (Array.isArray(d.beaten)) l.beaten = [...new Set(d.beaten.filter(x => typeof x === 'string' && x.length < 24))].slice(0, 64);
  if (d.daily && /^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d$/.test(d.daily.last)) l.daily = { last: d.daily.last, streak: whole(d.daily.streak, 7) };
  return l;
}
// puts a look on, if it's owned
function wear(l, k) { const it = ALL[k]; if (!it || !owns(l, k)) return false; l.wear[it.slot] = it.id; return true; }
function buyCase(l, grade) { const g = GRADES[grade]; if (!g) return 'unknown'; if (l.coins < g.price) return 'coins'; l.coins -= g.price; l.cases[grade]++; return ''; }
function buyKey(l, id) { const k = KEYS[id]; if (!k) return 'unknown'; if (l.coins < k.price) return 'coins'; l.coins -= k.price; l.keys[id]++; return ''; }

// Opens a case with a key: { item, dupe, coins } (the look won, whether it was owned already, the coins paid for a
// duplicate), or { why } when it can't be opened. The rarity comes from the case's odds; a rarity with nothing left to
// win in the key's slot gives way to the nearest one that has (rarer first), so a full shelf of commons stops coming
// up; within the rarity, looks you own are less likely than ones you don't (never, for a Super Lucky Box).
function open(l, grade, keyId, rnd) {
  const g = GRADES[grade], k = KEYS[keyId];
  if (!g || !k) return { why: 'unknown' };
  if (!l.cases[grade]) return { why: 'case' };
  if (!l.keys[keyId]) return { why: 'key' };
  let pool = ITEMS.filter(it => !it.free && (!k.slot || it.slot === k.slot));
  if (g.fresh && !pool.some(it => !owns(l, it.key))) pool = ITEMS.filter(it => !it.free);   // that slot is complete: any slot
  l.cases[grade]--; l.keys[keyId]--; l.opened++;
  const left = r => pool.filter(it => it.rarity === r && !owns(l, it.key)).length, any = r => pool.some(it => it.rarity === r);
  if (g.fresh && !RARITIES.some(left)) { l.coins += LUCKY_FULL; return { item: null, dupe: false, coins: LUCKY_FULL }; }
  let x = rnd() * 100, ri = 0;
  while (ri < 4 && x >= g.odds[ri]) x -= g.odds[ri++];
  if (l.pity >= PITY - 1 && ri < 2) ri = 2;
  // the nearest rarity with something to win (or, failing that, something at all): rarer first, then commoner
  const order = [ri, ...[1, 2, 3, 4].flatMap(d => [ri + d, ri - d])].filter(i => i >= 0 && i < 5);
  const pick = order.find(i => left(RARITIES[i])) ?? order.find(i => any(RARITIES[i]));
  const rarity = RARITIES[pick];
  l.pity = pick >= 2 ? 0 : l.pity + 1;
  const cands = pool.filter(it => it.rarity === rarity && !(g.fresh && owns(l, it.key))), w = cands.map(it => owns(l, it.key) ? OWNED_WEIGHT : 1);
  let y = rnd() * w.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), i = 0;
  while (i < cands.length - 1 && y >= w[i]) y -= w[i++];
  const item = cands[i], dupe = owns(l, item.key), coins = dupe ? RARITY[item.rarity].dupe : 0;
  l.owned[item.key] = count(l, item.key) + 1; l.coins += coins;
  return { item, dupe, coins };
}

// The end of a game against the computer: bot is { id, rating, top } (top: one of the three strongest), result 'win',
// 'draw' or 'loss'. Returns { coins, cases: [grades] } and adds them.
function gameOver(l, bot, result) {
  const out = { coins: 0, cases: [] };
  if (result === 'draw') out.coins = Math.round(winCoins(bot.rating) / 4);
  if (result === 'win') {
    out.coins = winCoins(bot.rating); for (let i = 0; i < WIN_CASES; i++) out.cases.push(gradeFor(bot.rating));
    if (!l.beaten.includes(bot.id)) { l.beaten.push(bot.id); out.cases.push(bot.top ? 'lucky' : better(gradeFor(bot.rating))); out.first = true; }
  }
  l.coins += out.coins; for (const g of out.cases) l.cases[g]++;
  return out;
}
// The day's gift, once a local date (YYYY-MM-DD): { coins, streak, lucky }, or null if it's been had today.
const nextDay = d => { const t = new Date(Date.UTC(+d.slice(0, 4), +d.slice(5, 7) - 1, +d.slice(8, 10) + 1)); return t.toISOString().slice(0, 10); };
function daily(l, day) {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d$/.test(day) || day === l.daily.last) return null;
  const streak = l.daily.last && nextDay(l.daily.last) === day && l.daily.streak < 7 ? l.daily.streak + 1 : 1;
  l.daily = { last: day, streak };
  const out = { coins: DAILY[streak - 1], streak, lucky: streak === 7 };
  l.coins += out.coins; if (out.lucky) l.cases.lucky++;
  return out;
}
// ------------------------------------------------------------------ trading
// An offer is what one player puts into a trade: { items: { 'slot:id': how many }, cases: { grade: n }, keys: { key: n },
// coins }. The free looks can't be traded: everyone has them. cleanOffer() makes a safe offer of anything (what the
// other player sent, say): only things that exist, in whole numbers.
const noOffer = () => ({ items: {}, cases: {}, keys: {}, coins: 0 });
function cleanOffer(o) {
  const out = noOffer();
  if (!o || typeof o !== 'object') return out;
  for (const it of ITEMS) { const n = whole(o.items && o.items[it.key], 999); if (n && !it.free) out.items[it.key] = n; }
  for (const g of GRADE_IDS) { const n = whole(o.cases && o.cases[g], 9999); if (n) out.cases[g] = n; }
  for (const k of KEY_IDS) { const n = whole(o.keys && o.keys[k], 9999); if (n) out.keys[k] = n; }
  out.coins = whole(o.coins);
  return out;
}
const offerEmpty = o => !o.coins && !Object.keys(o.items).length && !Object.keys(o.cases).length && !Object.keys(o.keys).length;
// an offer as one string, the same however it was put together, so two players can check they mean the same trade
const offerKey = o => JSON.stringify([Object.keys(o.items).sort().map(k => [k, o.items[k]]), GRADE_IDS.map(g => o.cases[g] || 0), KEY_IDS.map(k => o.keys[k] || 0), o.coins]);
// whether a locker holds everything in an offer
const canGive = (l, o) => l.coins >= o.coins && Object.keys(o.items).every(k => !ALL[k].free && count(l, k) >= o.items[k]) && Object.keys(o.cases).every(g => l.cases[g] >= o.cases[g]) && Object.keys(o.keys).every(k => l.keys[k] >= o.keys[k]);
// what a locker shows the other player in a trade: the looks, cases and keys that could be offered, never the coins
function inventory(l) { const items = {}; for (const it of ITEMS) if (!it.free && owns(l, it.key)) items[it.key] = count(l, it.key); return cleanOffer({ items, cases: l.cases, keys: l.keys }); }
// One side of a trade: the locker gives `give` and takes `get`. A look given away to the last copy comes off (its slot
// goes back to the free look). False, with nothing changed, if the locker doesn't hold what it's to give.
function trade(l, give, get) {
  if (!canGive(l, give)) return false;
  for (const k in give.items) { l.owned[k] -= give.items[k]; if (!l.owned[k]) { delete l.owned[k]; const it = ALL[k]; if (l.wear[it.slot] === it.id) l.wear[it.slot] = slotItems(it.slot)[0].id; } }
  for (const g in give.cases) l.cases[g] -= give.cases[g];
  for (const k in give.keys) l.keys[k] -= give.keys[k];
  for (const k in get.items) l.owned[k] = count(l, k) + get.items[k];
  for (const g in get.cases) l.cases[g] += get.cases[g];
  for (const k in get.keys) l.keys[k] += get.keys[k];
  l.coins += get.coins - give.coins;
  return true;
}
// how much of the catalogue a locker holds: { have, of } overall, or for one slot
function progress(l, slot) { const its = slot ? slotItems(slot) : ITEMS; return { have: its.filter(it => owns(l, it.key)).length, of: its.length }; }
// a locker with every look, plenty of coins, cases and keys (dev mode's test locker)
function full() { const l = fresh(); for (const it of ITEMS) l.owned[it.key] = 1; l.coins = 100000; for (const g of GRADE_IDS) l.cases[g] = 50; for (const k of KEY_IDS) l.keys[k] = 50; return l; }

return { SLOTS, RARITIES, RARITY, ITEMS, ALL, FREE, GRADES, GRADE_IDS, KEYS, KEY_IDS, OWNED_WEIGHT, PITY, LUCKY_FULL, DAILY, START,
  key, slotItems, winCoins, WIN_CASES, gradeFor, count, owns, fresh, validate, wear, buyCase, buyKey, open, gameOver, daily, nextDay, progress, full,
  noOffer, cleanOffer, offerEmpty, offerKey, canGive, inventory, trade };
})();
if (typeof module !== 'undefined') module.exports = LOOKS;
```

## src/sets.js

The piece sets (the shapes of the pieces, built from simple solids) and the finishes (what they are made of). Needs three.js, so browser only. (333 lines)

```js
// Retro Set's piece sets and finishes. A set is the shape of the six kinds of piece; a finish is what they're made of
// (the colours of the two sides, and how the surface takes the light). Any set can wear any finish. Every shape is
// built here from a few simple solids, in millimetres, as triangles in three slots: 0 the body, 1 the trim (collars,
// crowns, crosses and the like) and 2 the glow (lights, on the sets that have them). A finish gives each slot its
// material. Needs three.js (THREE), so it runs in the browser only; game.js uses it as SETS.
const SETS = (() => {
const T = THREE, PI = Math.PI;
const E = new T.Euler(), Q = new T.Quaternion(), M = new T.Matrix4(), V = new T.Vector3(), P0 = new T.Vector3(), S1 = new T.Vector3();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ the modelling kit
// maker() is a piece under construction. at: [x, y, z] of the solid's middle (a lathe's or slab's origin);
// rot: [rx, ry, rz]; sc: [sx, sy, sz]. Model space: y up, the piece's front towards -z (knights' heads towards +x).
function maker() {
  const tri = [[], [], []];
  const put = (g0, slot, at, rot, sc) => {
    const g = g0.index ? g0.toNonIndexed() : g0, p = g.attributes.position;
    E.set(...(rot || [0, 0, 0])); M.compose(P0.set(...(at || [0, 0, 0])), Q.setFromEuler(E), S1.set(...(sc || [1, 1, 1])));
    for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) { V.fromBufferAttribute(p, i).applyMatrix4(M); tri[slot].push(V.x, V.y, V.z); }
    return m;
  };
  const clip = (poly, nx, ny, nz, d, sg) => {   // the part of a polygon on one side of a plane
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < poly.length; i++) {
      const a = poly[i], b = poly[(i + 1) % poly.length];
      const da = sg * (a[0] * nx + a[1] * ny + a[2] * nz - d), db = sg * (b[0] * nx + b[1] * ny + b[2] * nz - d);
      if (da >= 0) out.push(a);
      if ((da >= 0) !== (db >= 0)) { const t = da / (da - db); out.push([a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * t]); }
    }
    return out;
  };
  const m = {
    tri,
    box: (w, h, d, at, slot = 0, rot) => put(new T.BoxGeometry(w, h, d), slot, at, rot),
    cyl: (r0, r1, h, n, at, slot = 0, rot) => put(new T.CylinderGeometry(r1, r0, h, n), slot, at, rot),   // r0 at the bottom, r1 at the top
    ball: (r, at, slot = 0, sc, w = 6, h = 4) => put(new T.SphereGeometry(r, w, h), slot, at, null, sc),
    gem: (r, at, slot = 0, sc, rot) => put(new T.OctahedronGeometry(r), slot, at, rot, sc),
    ring: (r, tube, n, at, slot = 0, rot = [PI / 2, 0, 0], arc = 2 * PI) => put(new T.TorusGeometry(r, tube, 4, n, arc), slot, at, rot),   // lying flat unless turned
    // a side view (points [x, y]) given thickness, centred on z
    slab(outline, depth, at, slot = 0, rot, bevel = 0) {
      const g = new T.ExtrudeGeometry(new T.Shape(outline.map(([x, y]) => new T.Vector2(x, y))), { depth, bevelEnabled: bevel > 0, bevelThickness: bevel, bevelSize: bevel * 0.85, bevelSegments: 1, curveSegments: 1 });
      g.translate(0, 0, -depth / 2);
      return put(g, slot, at, rot);
    },
    // a profile of [radius, height, slot of the band up to this point] turned round the upright, with n flat sides
    lathe(pts, n, slot = 0, at = [0, 0, 0], a0 = 0) {
      const [ox, oy, oz] = at;
      for (let i = 0; i + 1 < pts.length; i++) {
        const [r0, y0] = pts[i], [r1, y1, s] = pts[i + 1], t = tri[s === undefined ? slot : s];
        for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
          const a = a0 + j * 2 * PI / n, b = a0 + (j + 1) * 2 * PI / n, sa = Math.sin(a), ca = Math.cos(a), sb = Math.sin(b), cb = Math.cos(b);
          const A = [ox + r0 * sa, oy + y0, oz + r0 * ca], B = [ox + r0 * sb, oy + y0, oz + r0 * cb], Cc = [ox + r1 * sb, oy + y1, oz + r1 * cb], D = [ox + r1 * sa, oy + y1, oz + r1 * ca];
          if (r0 > 0) t.push(...A, ...B, ...D);
          if (r1 > 0) t.push(...B, ...Cc, ...D);
        }
      }
      return m;
    },
    // cuts away whatever lies inside every one of the planes ([nx, ny, nz, d]: inside is where n·p < d)
    carve(planes) {
      for (let s = 0; s < 3; s++) {
        const a = tri[s], keep = []; let rest = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i += 9) rest.push([[a[i], a[i + 1], a[i + 2]], [a[i + 3], a[i + 4], a[i + 5]], [a[i + 6], a[i + 7], a[i + 8]]]);
        for (const [nx, ny, nz, d] of planes) {
          const next = [];
          for (const poly of rest) { const o = clip(poly, nx, ny, nz, d, 1), n = clip(poly, nx, ny, nz, d, -1); if (o.length > 2) keep.push(o); if (n.length > 2) next.push(n); }
          rest = next;
        }
        tri[s] = [];
        for (const poly of keep) for (let i = 1; i + 1 < poly.length; i++) tri[s].push(...poly[0], ...poly[i], ...poly[i + 1]);
      }
      return m;
    },
    merge(o) { for (let s = 0; s < 3; s++) for (const v of o.tri[s]) tri[s].push(v); return m; },
    // nudges every corner a little (the same corner always the same way), for a hand-cut look; the foot stays flat
    jitter(amt) {
      for (const t of tri) for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i += 3) {
        if (t[i + 1] < 0.5) continue;
        let h = (Math.round(t[i] * 20) * 73856093) ^ (Math.round(t[i + 1] * 20) * 19349663) ^ (Math.round(t[i + 2] * 20) * 83492791);
        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 15), 2246822519) ^ (k + 1) * 374761393; t[i + k] += (((h >>> 8) & 1023) / 1023 - 0.5) * 2 * amt; }
      }
      return m;
    },
    geo() {
      const n = tri[0].length + tri[1].length + tri[2].length, pos = new Float32Array(n), g = new T.BufferGeometry();
      let o = 0;
      for (let s = 0; s < 3; s++) { if (tri[s].length) g.addGroup(o / 3, tri[s].length / 3, s); for (const v of tri[s]) pos[o++] = v * 0.001; }
      g.setAttribute('position', new T.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
      g.computeVertexNormals(); g.computeBoundingSphere(); g.computeBoundingBox();
      return g;
    },
  };
  return m;
}
// n things evenly round a circle of radius r: f(x, z, angle)
const round = (n, r, f, a0 = 0) => { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const a = a0 + i * 2 * PI / n; f(Math.sin(a) * r, Math.cos(a) * r, a); } };
// the bishop's cut: a slot sloping down into one side of the head (or, with no slope, a cleft straight down from the
// top to height y), with a smaller copy of the head inside so the cut shows a floor rather than a hole. head(k) makes
// the head at k times its width; w is half the cut's width.
function mitre(m, head, y, w, slope = 0.62) {
  const c = Math.cos(slope), s = Math.sin(slope), d = c * y - s * 2;   // the cut's middle passes through (2, y), rising outwards
  m.merge(head(maker(), 1).carve(slope ? [[-s, c, 0, d + w], [s, -c, 0, w - d], [-1, 0, 0, 1]] : [[1, 0, 0, w], [-1, 0, 0, w], [0, -1, 0, -y]]));
  return slope ? m.merge(head(maker(), 0.72)) : m.box(2 * w + 0.6, 1.2, 15, [0, y, 0]);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ classic
// Staunton shapes, each kind given one thing nothing else has: the pawn a plain ball, the bishop a tall pointed head
// with the cut, the rook battlements, the queen a ring of points, the king a big cross, the knight a horse's head.
const foot = rb => [[0, 0], [rb, 0], [rb, 4], [rb * 0.86, 6.5], [rb * 0.86, 9], [rb * 0.66, 12]];
const HORSE = [[-10, 0], [-11.5, 13], [-10, 27], [-7, 35], [-3.5, 41.5], [-1, 47], [1.5, 42], [6, 40], [13, 32], [17, 26.5], [16.5, 22], [10.5, 20.5], [5, 23.5], [6.5, 12], [10.5, 0]];
const classic = {
  P: m => m.lathe([...foot(14), [5, 21], [9.6, 23, 1], [9.6, 25.5, 1], [4.8, 27.5], [0, 27.5]], 12).ball(8.6, [0, 34, 0], 0, null, 8, 5),
  R: m => { m.lathe([...foot(16), [10.5, 16], [9.6, 34], [12.8, 37.5, 1], [12.8, 40, 1], [13.4, 41], [13.4, 45], [9.8, 45], [9.8, 42.5], [0, 42.5]], 12); round(6, 11.6, (x, z, a) => m.box(6.4, 5.5, 3.6, [x, 47.7, z], 0, [0, a, 0])); return m; },
  B: m => mitre(m.lathe([...foot(15.5), [8, 16.5], [5, 34], [10, 36, 1], [10, 38.5, 1], [5.4, 40]], 12),
    (h, k) => h.lathe([[5.4 * k, 40], [8.4 * k, 44], [9.8 * k, 50], [9 * k, 56.5], [6.2 * k, 62.5], [2.8 * k, 67.5], [0, 70.5]], 12), 55.5, 1.4).ball(2.6, [0, 71.8, 0], 1, null, 6, 4),
  Q: m => { m.lathe([...foot(18), [9.8, 17.5], [6, 44], [11, 46.5, 1], [11, 49, 1], [6.8, 50.5], [6.4, 54], [11.8, 66], [11.8, 67.5], [8, 67.5], [5, 71.5], [0, 72.5]], 12); round(8, 10.4, (x, z) => m.gem(2.4, [x, 69.6, z], 1, [1, 1.5, 1])); return m.ball(3.2, [0, 75.2, 0], 1, null, 6, 4); },
  K: m => m.lathe([...foot(19), [10.6, 18.5], [6.6, 50], [11.5, 52.5, 1], [11.5, 55, 1], [7.4, 56.5], [7, 60], [10.6, 72], [9.6, 74], [5, 75.5], [0, 75.5]], 12).box(4.4, 17, 4.4, [0, 84, 0], 1).box(14.5, 4.4, 4.4, [0, 86, 0], 1),
  N: m => m.lathe([...foot(16), [10.5, 16.5], [10.5, 19, 1], [0, 19, 1]], 12).slab(HORSE, 11, [0, 19, 0], 0, null, 1.2),
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ ornate
// The classic shapes dressed up: turned (baluster) stems, double collars, a ring of beads round every foot, a mane on
// the knight, an arched crown on the king. The trim carries most of it.
const ofoot = rb => [[0, 0], [rb, 0], [rb, 3], [rb * 0.9, 4.5], [rb * 0.9, 7, 1], [rb * 0.76, 8.5], [rb * 0.78, 11], [rb * 0.58, 14]];
const beads = (m, rb) => { round(12, rb * 0.92, (x, z) => m.gem(1.5, [x, 5.7, z], 1)); return m; };
const collars = (r, y, n) => [[r, y, 1], [r, y + 1.6, 1], [r * 0.62, y + 2.6], [r * 0.84, y + 3.6, 1], [r * 0.84, y + 4.8, 1], [n, y + 5.8]];
const MANE = [[-10.5, 12], [-14, 15], [-12, 19], [-15, 23], [-12.5, 27], [-14.5, 31], [-11, 34], [-12, 38.5], [-7.5, 40], [-7.5, 44.5], [-3, 44], [-3.5, 41.5], [-7, 35], [-10, 27], [-11.5, 13]];
const ornate = {
  P: m => beads(m, 14.5).lathe([...ofoot(14.5), [5.4, 17], [7.4, 20.5], [4.6, 24.5], ...collars(9.6, 26, 4.6), [0, 31.8]], 12).ball(8, [0, 38.2, 0], 0, null, 8, 5).ring(8.1, 0.9, 10, [0, 38.2, 0], 1),
  R: m => { beads(m, 16.5).lathe([...ofoot(16.5), [10.6, 15], [9, 22], [9.6, 24, 1], [9.6, 26, 1], [9, 28], [9.8, 36], [13, 40, 1], [13.8, 42, 1], [13.8, 47.5], [10.4, 47.5], [10.4, 44], [0, 44]], 12); round(8, 12.1, (x, z, a) => m.box(5, 5.5, 3.4, [x, 50.2, z], 1, [0, a, 0])); return m; },
  B: m => mitre(beads(m, 16).lathe([...ofoot(16), [6, 17], [8.2, 22], [4.8, 30], [4.6, 37], ...collars(10, 39, 5.4)], 12),
    (h, k) => h.lathe([[5.4 * k, 44.8], [7.6 * k, 48], [9 * k, 53, k === 1 ? 1 : 0], [9 * k, 55, k === 1 ? 1 : 0], [8 * k, 60], [5.4 * k, 66], [2.6 * k, 71], [0, 73.5]], 12), 61, 1.3).ball(2.6, [0, 74.8, 0], 1, null, 6, 4).gem(1.6, [0, 78.2, 0], 1, [1, 1.8, 1]),
  Q: m => {
    beads(m, 18.5).lathe([...ofoot(18.5), [6.8, 18], [9.4, 24], [5.4, 34], [5.2, 44], ...collars(11, 46, 6.6), [6.2, 55.5], [9, 62], [12.2, 68, 1], [12.2, 70, 1], [8.4, 70], [5.2, 74.5, 1], [0, 75.5, 1]], 12);
    round(8, 11, (x, z, a) => { m.cyl(1.9, 0.5, 6.5, 4, [x * 1.04, 73.2, z * 1.04], 1, [0, a, 0]); m.ball(1.5, [x * 1.07, 77.3, z * 1.07], 1, null, 5, 3); });
    return m.ball(3.3, [0, 78.4, 0], 1, null, 6, 4).gem(1.7, [0, 83, 0], 1, [1, 1.7, 1]);
  },
  K: m => {
    beads(m, 19.5).lathe([...ofoot(19.5), [7.4, 18.5], [10, 25], [5.8, 36], [5.6, 49], ...collars(11.6, 51, 7.2), [7, 60.5], [10.8, 70, 1], [11.4, 72, 1], [11.4, 74.5, 1], [10, 75.5], [6, 79], [0, 80.5]], 12);
    round(4, 7.2, (x, z, a) => m.box(2, 2, 11, [x, 80.2, z], 1, [0.62, a, 0]), PI / 4);   // the crown's arches
    round(8, 11.5, (x, z) => m.gem(1.5, [x, 76.6, z], 1, [1, 1.6, 1]));
    return m.ball(3.2, [0, 85.2, 0], 1, null, 6, 4).box(3.6, 13, 3.6, [0, 93.5, 0], 1).box(12, 3.6, 3.6, [0, 94.5, 0], 1).box(5.6, 2.2, 4, [0, 99.6, 0], 1).box(2.2, 5.6, 4, [-5.6, 94.5, 0], 1).box(2.2, 5.6, 4, [5.6, 94.5, 0], 1);
  },
  N: m => beads(m, 16.5).lathe([...ofoot(16.5), [10.6, 16], [11.4, 17.5, 1], [11.4, 19.5, 1], [10, 21], [0, 21]], 12).slab(HORSE, 11, [0, 21, 0], 0, null, 1.2).slab(MANE, 5, [0, 21, 0], 1)
    .box(3.2, 1.6, 12.6, [10.5, 49.6, 0], 1, [0, 0, -0.9]).box(1.6, 9, 12.6, [5.6, 53, 0], 1, [0, 0, 0.5]),   // the bridle: a noseband and a cheek strap
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ minimal
// Plain turned forms with no collars or mouldings: each kind is one clean shape and one mark on top.
const minimal = {
  P: m => m.lathe([[0, 0], [11, 0], [6, 21], [0, 21]], 16).ball(8.4, [0, 26, 0], 0, null, 10, 6),
  R: m => { m.lathe([[0, 0], [13, 0], [13, 37], [0, 37]], 16); for (const [x, z] of [[1, 1], [1, -1], [-1, 1], [-1, -1]]) m.box(8.6, 7, 8.6, [x * 6.3, 40.5, z * 6.3]); return m; },
  B: m => mitre(m.lathe([[0, 0], [12, 0], [7, 40]], 16), (h, k) => h.lathe([[7 * k, 40], [9.4 * k, 46], [8.6 * k, 54], [4.6 * k, 62], [0, 68]], 16), 53.5, 1.7),
  Q: m => m.lathe([[0, 0], [13, 0], [6, 50], [13.6, 63], [13.6, 64.5], [10.5, 64.5], [0, 59]], 16).ball(5.6, [0, 65, 0], 1, null, 10, 6),
  K: m => m.lathe([[0, 0], [14, 0], [6.6, 56], [10.5, 66], [10.5, 68], [0, 68]], 16).box(5, 17, 5, [0, 76.5, 0], 1).box(15, 5, 5, [0, 78, 0], 1),
  N: m => m.lathe([[0, 0], [13, 0], [12.4, 6], [0, 6]], 16).slab([[-10, 0], [-10, 30], [-3.5, 50], [5, 48], [15.5, 36], [14, 28], [5.5, 30], [7, 0]], 14, [0, 6, 0]),
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ blocks
// Every piece stacked from little cubes (4.4 mm), like 3D pixel art. A layer is [width, how many cubes high, slot].
const U = 4.4;
const stack = (m, layers, y = 0) => { for (const [w, n = 1, s = 0] of layers) { m.box(w * U, n * U, w * U, [0, (y + n / 2) * U, 0], s); y += n; } return y; };
const cubes = (m, list, s = 0) => { for (const [x, y, z, w = 1, h = 1, d = w] of list) m.box(w * U, h * U, d * U, [x * U, (y + h / 2) * U, z * U], s); return m; };
const NAG = ['..#....', '.###...', '.####..', '.#####.', '.######', '.###.##', '.###...', '.####..', '.####..'];   // the knight from the side, top row first
const blocks = {
  P: m => { stack(m, [[5], [3, 2], [5, 1, 1], [3], [5, 3]]); return m; },
  R: m => { const y = stack(m, [[7], [5, 5], [7, 1, 1], [7]]); return cubes(m, [[2.5, y, 2.5, 2, 1.5], [2.5, y, -2.5, 2, 1.5], [-2.5, y, 2.5, 2, 1.5], [-2.5, y, -2.5, 2, 1.5]]); },
  B: m => { const y = stack(m, [[7], [5], [3, 5], [5, 1, 1], [3]]); cubes(m, [[-1.5, y, 0, 2, 3, 5], [1.5, y, 0, 2, 3, 5], [0, y, 0, 1, 1, 5]]); stack(m, [[3, 2], [1, 2, 1]], y + 3); return m; },   // the cut: a gap down the head
  Q: m => { const y = stack(m, [[7], [5], [3, 8], [5, 1, 1], [3], [5], [7]]); cubes(m, [[3, y, 3], [3, y, -3], [-3, y, 3], [-3, y, -3], [3, y, 0], [-3, y, 0], [0, y, 3], [0, y, -3]], 1); stack(m, [[3], [1, 2, 1]], y); return m; },
  K: m => { const y = stack(m, [[7], [5], [3, 9], [5, 1, 1], [3], [5], [7, 2]]); return cubes(m, [[0, y, 0, 1, 5], [0, y + 2.5, 0, 3, 1, 1]], 1); },
  N: m => {
    const y = stack(m, [[7], [5]]);
    NAG.forEach((row, j) => { for (let a = 0; a < 7; a++) { if (row[a] !== '#') continue; let b = a; while (row[b + 1] === '#') b++; cubes(m, [[(a + b) / 2 - 3, y + NAG.length - 1 - j, 0, b - a + 1, 1, 3]]); a = b; } });
    return cubes(m, [[1, y + 5, 0, 1, 1, 3.4]], 1);   // an eye
  },
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ figures
// Little medieval people on round stands: a foot soldier with shield and spear, a castle tower with a flag, a rider on
// a horse, a bishop with mitre and crook, a queen in a wide gown, a king with crown, cape and sword.
const stand = (m, rb, sx = 1) => { const s = maker().lathe([[0, 0], [rb, 0], [rb, 3], [rb * 0.86, 4.5], [0, 4.5]], 10); for (let i = 0; i < s.tri[0].length; i += 3) s.tri[0][i] *= sx; return m.merge(s); };
const figures = {
  P: m => stand(m, 13).lathe([[8, 4.5], [6, 17], [6.6, 23], [3, 25.5], [0, 25.5]], 8).ball(5, [0, 29.5, 0])
    .cyl(7.4, 7, 1.3, 8, [0, 32.4, 0], 1).cyl(5.2, 0.6, 5.6, 8, [0, 35.6, 0], 1)                    // a kettle helmet
    .box(9.5, 12, 1.6, [-2.5, 15.5, -7], 1, [0.12, 0, 0]).gem(2, [-2.5, 15.5, -8], 0, [1, 1, 0.5])   // the shield and its boss
    .box(1.4, 33, 1.4, [8.2, 21, -1.5]).gem(1.9, [8.2, 39.5, -1.5], 1, [1, 2.2, 1]),               // the spear
  R: m => {
    stand(m, 16).lathe([[12.4, 4.5], [10.6, 37], [13.4, 40.5, 1], [13.4, 45], [0, 45]], 8, 0, [0, 0, 0], PI / 8);
    round(4, 11.4, (x, z, a) => m.box(8.4, 6, 4.4, [x, 48, z], 0, [0, a, 0]));
    m.box(6.4, 11, 1.6, [0, 10, -11.4], 1).box(1.6, 6, 1.6, [0, 28, -10.6], 1).box(1.6, 6, 1.6, [10.6, 28, 0], 1).box(1.6, 6, 1.6, [-10.6, 28, 0], 1);   // the door and arrow slits
    return m.box(1.3, 15, 1.3, [0, 52.5, 0]).box(8, 5, 0.9, [4.4, 57.2, 0], 1);                      // the flag
  },
  N: m => stand(m, 15.5, 1.18)
    .box(25, 10.5, 9.5, [0, 23.2, 0]).box(13.5, 7, 10.4, [-4, 24.4, 0], 1)                           // the horse and its cloth
    .box(3.4, 14, 3.4, [9, 11.5, 3]).box(3.4, 14, 3.4, [9, 11.5, -3]).box(3.4, 14, 3.4, [-9.5, 11.5, 3]).box(3.4, 14, 3.4, [-9.5, 11.5, -3])
    .box(6.4, 15, 6.6, [12.4, 32.4, 0], 0, [0, 0, -0.5]).box(11, 6, 6, [18.4, 38.4, 0], 0, [0, 0, -0.42])   // neck and head
    .box(1.8, 4, 1.6, [14.6, 43.6, 2]).box(1.8, 4, 1.6, [14.6, 43.6, -2]).box(1.8, 12, 5.6, [9.4, 36, 0], 1, [0, 0, -0.5])   // ears, mane
    .box(3, 11, 3, [-14, 22.4, 0], 0, [0, 0, -0.45])                                                 // tail
    .box(7, 12, 8.4, [-3.4, 34.4, 0]).ball(4.2, [-3.4, 44.2, 0]).cyl(4.9, 4.4, 4.4, 6, [-3.4, 46.6, 0], 1).gem(1.8, [-3.4, 51, 0], 1, [1, 2, 1])   // the rider, helmet and plume
    .box(8.4, 10, 1.5, [-3, 33.4, -5.4], 1).box(1.3, 34, 1.3, [1, 40, 5.4], 1, [0, 0, -0.5]),          // shield and lance
  B: m => stand(m, 14.5).lathe([[10, 4.5], [7, 30], [6.6, 41], [7.8, 44], [3.6, 47.5], [0, 47.5]], 8).ball(5.2, [0, 51.6, 0])
    .gem(6.4, [0, 59.4, 0], 1, [1, 1.3, 0.56]).box(1.6, 11, 4, [0, 58.8, 0], 0)                      // the mitre and its band
    .box(3.2, 33, 1.2, [0, 25.5, -7.4], 1, [-0.085, 0, 0])                                           // a stole down the front
    .box(1.5, 51, 1.5, [10, 30, -1]).ring(3.6, 0.95, 7, [6.4, 55.5, -1], 1, [0, 0, -0.6], 1.55 * PI),   // the crook
  Q: m => {
    stand(m, 16).lathe([[13.4, 4.5], [12.2, 10, 1], [5.4, 42], [7.6, 50], [3.8, 54], [0, 54]], 10).ball(5.4, [0, 58.9, 0]);
    m.box(10.4, 17, 3.4, [0, 53.5, 5]).ring(6.4, 1, 8, [0, 51.1, 0], 1);                             // hair, a necklace
    m.cyl(5, 5.6, 3.4, 8, [0, 64.5, 0], 1); round(5, 5, (x, z) => m.gem(1.5, [x, 67.9, z], 1, [1, 1.9, 1]));
    return m;
  },
  K: m => {
    stand(m, 16.5).lathe([[12.6, 4.5], [8.6, 45], [10.2, 52], [10.2, 56, 1], [4.6, 59.5, 1], [0, 59.5]], 8).ball(6, [0, 64.6, 0]);
    m.box(15.5, 45, 2.2, [0, 31, 8.2], 1, [0.1, 0, 0]).box(6.6, 5.6, 3, [0, 60.6, -4.6]);             // a cape, a beard
    m.cyl(6.4, 7.2, 5, 8, [0, 71.4, 0], 1); round(4, 6.5, (x, z) => m.gem(1.7, [x, 75.4, z], 1, [1, 1.9, 1]), PI / 4);
    m.box(2, 9, 2, [0, 78.4, 0], 1).box(6.4, 2, 2, [0, 79.6, 0], 1);
    return m.box(2.2, 27, 1.3, [0, 18, -11.4], 0, [-0.1, 0, 0]).box(9, 1.8, 1.8, [0, 32.4, -10], 1).box(1.8, 5, 1.8, [0, 35.6, -9.7], 1);   // the sword
  },
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ sci-fi
// Six-sided machines with lights (the glow slot): a hovering drone, a gun turret, a walker's head, a signal spire with
// a tilted ring, a queen with a ring of spikes round a floating core, a king with an aerial and a halo.
const pad = (m, rb) => m.lathe([[0, 0], [rb, 0], [rb, 2.5], [rb * 0.8, 5], [rb * 0.8, 6.5, 2], [rb * 0.55, 6.5], [0, 6.5]], 6);
const scifi = {
  P: m => pad(m, 13).cyl(2.6, 2.6, 14, 6, [0, 13.5, 0], 1).lathe([[0, 18], [9, 22], [10.4, 25], [10.4, 27.5, 2], [9, 30.5], [5, 34], [0, 34]], 6),
  R: m => {
    pad(m, 16).lathe([[13, 6.5], [11, 22], [11.2, 25, 2], [10.6, 30], [13.6, 33.5, 1], [13.6, 39], [10.6, 39], [10.6, 36.5], [0, 36.5]], 6);
    round(6, 12.2, (x, z, a) => m.box(4.2, 6.5, 3.4, [x, 42.2, z], 0, [0, a, 0]), PI / 6);
    return m.cyl(2, 2, 4, 6, [0, 38.5, 0], 2);
  },
  N: m => pad(m, 15).cyl(3.4, 3.4, 9, 6, [-2, 11, 0], 1)
    .slab([[-9, 0], [-12, 20], [-6, 34], [3, 39], [19, 30], [20, 25], [8.5, 21.5], [4, 13], [7, 0]], 11, [0, 14, 0], 0, null, 1)
    .slab([[-10, 22], [-17, 40], [-4.5, 35.5]], 3, [0, 14, 0], 1).box(4.6, 2.6, 13.6, [9, 44.6, 0], 2, [0, 0, -0.52]),   // a fin and the eye
  B: m => pad(m, 14).lathe([[9, 6.5], [4.6, 30], [4.4, 42], [0, 42]], 6).lathe([[0, 41], [6.6, 50], [3.4, 62, 1], [0, 71, 2]], 6)
    .ring(10.5, 1.1, 6, [0, 49, 0], 2, [PI / 2 - 0.5, 0, 0.42]),
  Q: m => {
    pad(m, 17).lathe([[11, 6.5], [5.4, 44], [5.6, 46.5, 2], [9, 54], [12, 58, 1], [12, 60, 1], [7, 60], [0, 57]], 6);
    round(6, 10.6, (x, z, a) => m.cyl(1.9, 0.3, 13, 4, [x * 1.12, 65.6, z * 1.12], 1, [0.22, a, 0]), PI / 6);
    return m.gem(4.8, [0, 68, 0], 2, [1, 1.35, 1]);
  },
  K: m => {
    pad(m, 18).lathe([[12, 6.5], [6.4, 48], [6.6, 50.5, 2], [10, 58], [11.4, 61, 1], [11.4, 66, 1], [8, 68], [0, 68]], 6);
    m.box(3.2, 23, 3.2, [0, 79.5, 0], 1).box(14.5, 3.2, 3.2, [0, 82, 0], 1);
    m.gem(2.2, [0, 92.4, 0], 2).gem(2.2, [8.6, 82, 0], 2).gem(2.2, [-8.6, 82, 0], 2);
    return m.ring(10.4, 1.1, 6, [0, 72.5, 0], 2);
  },
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ carved
// Short, heavy pieces with eight flat sides, every corner cut a little unevenly as if by hand.
const cfoot = rb => [[0, 0], [rb, 0], [rb, 5], [rb * 0.8, 8]];
const NAG_C = [[-12, 0], [-13.5, 14], [-10.5, 26], [-4.5, 32], [-1, 37.5], [2.5, 32], [9, 30], [17, 22], [17, 15.5], [10, 13], [6, 16.5], [8.5, 0]];
const carved = {
  P: m => m.lathe([...cfoot(14), [7.6, 13], [10.4, 15, 1], [10.4, 17.5, 1], [6.4, 19], [0, 19]], 8).ball(8.6, [0, 25.2, 0], 0, null, 8, 4),
  R: m => { m.lathe([...cfoot(17), [12.6, 24], [15.6, 27.5, 1], [15.6, 32], [0, 32]], 8, 0, [0, 0, 0], PI / 8); round(4, 11.8, (x, z, a) => m.box(10.5, 7, 6, [x, 35.5, z], 0, [0, a, 0])); return m; },
  B: m => mitre(m.lathe([...cfoot(16), [9, 24], [11.6, 26, 1], [11.6, 28.8, 1], [8.4, 30]], 8),
    (h, k) => h.lathe([[8.4 * k, 30], [11.4 * k, 36], [11.4 * k, 44], [9.4 * k, 53], [5.4 * k, 60], [0, 61]], 8), 45, 3, 0),   // cleft straight down: two horns
  Q: m => { m.lathe([...cfoot(17), [9.4, 30], [12.6, 32.5, 1], [12.6, 35, 1], [9, 36.5], [14, 49], [14, 51], [8.5, 51], [0, 54]], 8); round(8, 12.2, (x, z) => m.gem(3, [x, 52.6, z], 1, [1, 1.4, 1]), PI / 8); return m.ball(4.6, [0, 58.4, 0], 1, null, 6, 4); },
  K: m => m.lathe([...cfoot(18), [10.4, 36], [13.6, 38.5, 1], [13.6, 41.2, 1], [10, 42.6], [12.8, 52], [11.6, 54.5], [0, 54.5]], 8).box(6, 16, 6, [0, 62.5, 0], 1).box(16, 5.6, 6, [0, 64, 0], 1),
  N: m => m.lathe([...cfoot(16.5), [13, 9.5], [0, 9.5]], 8).slab(NAG_C, 15, [0, 9.5, 0], 0, null, 1.6),
};
const ROUGH = { carved: 0.55 };   // how far each corner is nudged, in mm
const STRETCH = { figures: [1.25, 1.06, 1.25], figuresN: [1.08, 1.06, 1.25] };   // the little people made stockier, so they read from above (the horse less so: it's long already)

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ the lists
const LIST = [
  { id: 'classic', name: 'Classic', make: classic }, { id: 'ornate', name: 'Ornate', make: ornate }, { id: 'minimal', name: 'Minimal', make: minimal },
  { id: 'blocks', name: 'Blocks', make: blocks }, { id: 'figures', name: 'Figures', make: figures }, { id: 'scifi', name: 'Sci-fi', make: scifi },
  { id: 'carved', name: 'Carved', make: carved },
];
const byId = id => LIST.find(s => s.id === id) || LIST[0];
const GEO = {};
// the shape of one kind of piece ('P', 'N', 'B', 'R', 'Q' or 'K') in a set, made once
function geo(set, kind) {
  const s = byId(set), key = s.id + kind;
  if (!GEO[key]) {
    const m = s.make[kind](maker()), k = STRETCH[key] || STRETCH[s.id];
    if (k) for (const t of m.tri) for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i++) t[i] *= k[i % 3];
    if (ROUGH[s.id]) m.jitter(ROUGH[s.id]);
    GEO[key] = m.geo();
  }
  return GEO[key];
}

// Finishes. For each side (w, b): the body, trim and glow colours, and dark, a little light of its own so a dark piece
// never goes flat black. lit and trimLit: how much of the glow's and the trim's colour shines by itself (0 to 1);
// shine: a polished surface that catches the lamp (none for matt), gloss how tight its highlights are, spec their colour;
// anim: a finish that moves (tick()). The ids and names match the Finish slot in looks.js.
const FINISHES = [
  { id: 'classic', name: 'Classic', w: { body: '#efe1c3', trim: '#b98a4e', glow: '#ffc56b' }, b: { body: '#4b446e', trim: '#d9b877', glow: '#6cb8ff', dark: '#0c0a18' } },
  { id: 'wood', name: 'Wood', w: { body: '#edd6a6', trim: '#a8743c', glow: '#ffe9a8' }, b: { body: '#6a3c22', trim: '#2e1a10', glow: '#ff9a4a', dark: '#120905' } },
  { id: 'chalk', name: 'Chalk', w: { body: '#f4f1ea', trim: '#c9c2b4', glow: '#ffffff' }, b: { body: '#474b54', trim: '#25272d', glow: '#aab4c4', dark: '#0a0b0d' } },
  { id: 'sea', name: 'Sea', w: { body: '#e6f4f7', trim: '#7fc4d8', glow: '#bff2ff' }, b: { body: '#24597f', trim: '#8fd0e8', glow: '#6fe0ff', dark: '#061018' } },
  { id: 'forest', name: 'Forest', w: { body: '#f1ead0', trim: '#8a9a4a', glow: '#e0f0a0' }, b: { body: '#34633a', trim: '#c9b060', glow: '#b8f08a', dark: '#07100a' } },
  { id: 'marble', name: 'Marble', shine: 0.22, w: { body: '#eceae4', trim: '#9aa3ad', glow: '#bfe6ff' }, b: { body: '#3f5550', trim: '#cfae6a', glow: '#8ff0c8', dark: '#07100e' } },
  { id: 'metal', name: 'Metal', shine: 0.6, w: { body: '#c4cbd6', trim: '#eef2f8', glow: '#8fd8ff', gloss: 24 }, b: { body: '#7a5230', trim: '#d9a94a', glow: '#ffb060', gloss: 24, spec: '#ffd9a0', dark: '#140c05' } },
  { id: 'ember', name: 'Ember', w: { body: '#f6e6cf', trim: '#d9703a', glow: '#ffc08a' }, b: { body: '#822424', trim: '#ffb347', glow: '#ff7a3a', dark: '#160505' } },
  { id: 'candy', name: 'Candy', w: { body: '#ffd9e6', trim: '#ffffff', glow: '#fff0a8' }, b: { body: '#2f8578', trim: '#d6fff2', glow: '#a8fff0', dark: '#081816' } },
  { id: 'neon', name: 'Neon', lit: 0.9, trimLit: 0.55, w: { body: '#cfd6e6', trim: '#35e0ff', glow: '#b8fbff' }, b: { body: '#2a2140', trim: '#ff4fb4', glow: '#ffb3e6', dark: '#0b0814' } },
  { id: 'ice', name: 'Ice', shine: 0.5, w: { body: '#eaf7ff', trim: '#9fdcff', glow: '#ffffff', gloss: 60 }, b: { body: '#2c5490', trim: '#bfe6ff', glow: '#8fd8ff', gloss: 60, dark: '#060c18' } },
  { id: 'obsidian', name: 'Obsidian', shine: 0.7, w: { body: '#e9dfc9', trim: '#b7a27a', glow: '#fff0c8', gloss: 50 }, b: { body: '#2f2a3c', trim: '#9288b4', glow: '#c8b8ff', gloss: 80, spec: '#cfc8ff', dark: '#0a0810' } },
  { id: 'royal', name: 'Royal', shine: 0.25, trimLit: 0.25, w: { body: '#f7efdc', trim: '#e0b040', glow: '#fff0a0' }, b: { body: '#45246c', trim: '#f0c048', glow: '#ffd870', dark: '#0c0616' } },
  { id: 'circuit', name: 'Circuit', lit: 0.9, trimLit: 0.5, w: { body: '#d9dfd6', trim: '#2fd27a', glow: '#8fffc0' }, b: { body: '#24322a', trim: '#39ff8a', glow: '#b8ffd6', dark: '#060a08' } },
  { id: 'rainbow', name: 'Rainbow', anim: 'rainbow', lit: 0.8, trimLit: 0.5, w: { body: '#f6f2ff', trim: '#ff6f8f', glow: '#ffc56b' }, b: { body: '#2d2746', trim: '#6cb8ff', glow: '#7dffb0', dark: '#0b0814' } },
  { id: 'molten', name: 'Molten', anim: 'molten', lit: 0.9, trimLit: 0.6, w: { body: '#ffe9c4', trim: '#ffb347', glow: '#fff0a0' }, b: { body: '#33191a', trim: '#ff5a1a', glow: '#ffb060', dark: '#120605' } },
];
const finish = id => FINISHES.find(f => f.id === id) || FINISHES[0];
const MATS = {};
function mats(fin, c) {
  const f = finish(fin), key = f.id + c;
  if (!MATS[key]) {
    const d = f[c], shine = d.shine === undefined ? f.shine || 0 : d.shine;
    const mk = (col, glow) => {
      const o = { color: col, flatShading: true, side: T.DoubleSide };
      if (glow) o.emissive = new T.Color(col).multiplyScalar(glow); else if (d.dark) o.emissive = d.dark;
      return shine ? new T.MeshPhongMaterial({ ...o, specular: new T.Color(d.spec || '#ffffff').multiplyScalar(shine), shininess: d.gloss || 40 }) : new T.MeshLambertMaterial(o);
    };
    MATS[key] = [mk(d.body), mk(d.trim, f.trimLit), mk(d.glow, f.lit === undefined ? 0.5 : f.lit)];
  }
  return MATS[key];
}
// The two finishes that move: Rainbow's trim and lights drift round the colours (the two sides half a turn apart), and
// Molten's glow swells and fades. Slowly: nothing changes faster than about once every three seconds. t in seconds.
function tick(t) {
  for (const key in MATS) {
    const f = finish(key.slice(0, -1)), c = key.slice(-1); if (!f.anim) continue;
    const [, trim, glow] = MATS[key];
    if (f.anim === 'rainbow') { const h = (t * 0.05 + (c === 'b' ? 0.5 : 0)) % 1; trim.color.setHSL(h, 0.85, 0.6); trim.emissive.setHSL(h, 0.85, 0.3); glow.color.setHSL((h + 0.15) % 1, 0.9, 0.7); glow.emissive.setHSL((h + 0.15) % 1, 0.9, 0.5); }
    else { const p = 0.5 + 0.35 * Math.sin(t * 1.3 + (c === 'b' ? 2 : 0)); trim.emissive.set(f[c].trim).multiplyScalar(p); glow.emissive.set(f[c].glow).multiplyScalar(Math.min(1, p + 0.25)); }
  }
}
return { LIST, FINISHES, byId, finish, geo, mats, tick };
})();
```

## src/game.js

Everything in the browser: rendering, camera, menus and the installable app. (2015 lines)

```js
// Retro Set in the browser: rendering, the board and pieces, input, camera, the game screens, saving and the
// installable app. The rules themselves are in core.js (C here); this file only shows them and asks them.
(() => {
'use strict';
const C = CORE;
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const clamp = (v, a, b) => v < a ? a : v > b ? b : v;
const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
const ease = t => t < 0.5 ? 2 * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 2) / 2;
const wrapA = a => { while (a > Math.PI) a -= 2 * Math.PI; while (a < -Math.PI) a += 2 * Math.PI; return a; };
const BUILD = '__BUILD__';   // version: tools/build.js fills in a fingerprint of the game code
const reduceMotion = window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ settings
// pixel: game pixels are this many screen pixels across (0 is Max: one per real screen pixel); levels: shades per
// colour channel (256 is full colour; fewer turns on the dithering); turn: in a game on one device, the view turns
// round to the player whose move it is; set and finish: the pieces (sets.js); hints: dots and rings where a picked-up
// piece can go; autoQueen: a pawn reaching the end becomes a queen without asking; confirm: a move waits for a tap on
// Confirm; flat: in a game, look straight down at flat pictures of the pieces; coords: the letters and numbers round
// the board; lastMove: the last move's squares tinted; volume: 0 to 1 (0.75 the loudness the sounds were made at);
// vibrate: a buzz for a capture and for check, on phones that can; mySet: online, both sides wear your pieces
const DEFAULTS = { pixel: 1, levels: 256, dither: true, outline: true, turn: true, set: 'classic', finish: 'classic',
  hints: true, autoQueen: false, confirm: false, flat: false, coords: true, lastMove: true, volume: 0.75, vibrate: true, mySet: false };
const S = { ...DEFAULTS };
try { Object.assign(S, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('retroSet.settings') || '{}')); } catch (e) {}
S.set = SETS.byId(S.set).id; S.finish = SETS.finish(S.finish).id;
if (!(S.volume >= 0 && S.volume <= 1)) S.volume = DEFAULTS.volume;
// what is worn in each of the locker's seven slots (looks.js): the pieces' set and finish are S.set and S.finish
const W = { board: 'standard', room: 'parlour', fx: 'none', avatar: 'plain', marks: 'standard' };
const saveS = () => { try { localStorage.setItem('retroSet.settings', JSON.stringify(S)); } catch (e) {} };

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ renderer + post
// The scene is drawn small into a render target, then a shader adds outlines where the depth jumps, a vignette and,
// below full colour, Bayer dithering; the canvas is scaled up with hard pixel edges. The same pipeline as Retro Rack.
const canvas = $('#view');
const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: false, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
THREE.ColorManagement.enabled = false;
renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.LinearSRGBColorSpace;
renderer.setPixelRatio(1);
renderer.info.autoReset = false;   // counted per frame (see the main loop), so the tests see the scene, not just the last pass
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
const ROOM = new THREE.Color('#1a1433');
scene.background = ROOM;
scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(ROOM, 4, 11);
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(40, 1, 0.03, 12);   // seeing no further than the fog: the floor fades out with no edge for the outlines to find

const postMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
  uniforms: {
    tColor: { value: null }, tDepth: { value: null }, uRes: { value: new THREE.Vector2(1, 1) },
    uLevels: { value: 8 }, uDither: { value: 1 }, uOutline: { value: 1 }, uNear: { value: camera.near }, uFar: { value: camera.far },
  },
  vertexShader: 'varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = uv; gl_Position = vec4(position.xy, 0.0, 1.0); }',
  fragmentShader: `
    uniform sampler2D tColor; uniform sampler2D tDepth; uniform vec2 uRes;
    uniform float uLevels, uDither, uOutline, uNear, uFar;
    varying vec2 vUv;
    float linZ(float d){ return uNear*uFar/(uFar - d*(uFar-uNear)); }
    float b2(vec2 v){ return mod(2.0*v.x + 3.0*v.y, 4.0); }
    float bayer4(vec2 p){ vec2 q = mod(floor(p), 4.0); return (4.0*b2(mod(q,2.0)) + b2(floor(q/2.0)) + 0.5)/16.0; }
    void main(){
      vec2 px = 1.0/uRes;
      vec3 col = texture2D(tColor, vUv).rgb;
      if (uOutline > 0.5) {
        float dc = texture2D(tDepth, vUv).x;
        float dl = texture2D(tDepth, vUv - vec2(px.x,0.0)).x, dr = texture2D(tDepth, vUv + vec2(px.x,0.0)).x;
        float dd = texture2D(tDepth, vUv - vec2(0.0,px.y)).x, du = texture2D(tDepth, vUv + vec2(0.0,px.y)).x;
        float zc = linZ(dc);
        float lap = abs(dl + dr - 2.0*dc) + abs(du + dd - 2.0*dc);
        float dz = lap * zc*zc*(uFar-uNear)/(uNear*uFar);
        float zmax = max(max(linZ(dl),linZ(dr)), max(linZ(du),linZ(dd)));
        float edge = step(0.02*zc + 0.012, dz) * step(0.03*zc + 0.015, zmax - zc);
        if (dc >= 0.99999) edge = 0.0;
        col = mix(col, col*0.22 + vec3(0.035,0.025,0.07), edge*0.9);
      }
      vec2 c = vUv - 0.5; col *= 1.0 - 0.55*dot(c,c);
      if (uLevels < 200.0) {
        float t = (bayer4(gl_FragCoord.xy) - 0.5) * uDither;
        col = floor(col*(uLevels-1.0) + 0.5 + t) / (uLevels-1.0);
      }
      gl_FragColor = vec4(clamp(col,0.0,1.0), 1.0);
    }`,
  depthTest: false, depthWrite: false,
});
const postScene = new THREE.Scene();
postScene.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2), postMat));
const postCam = new THREE.OrthographicCamera(-1, 1, 1, -1, 0, 1);
let rt = null, VW = 1, VH = 1;
// Pixel size 0 is Max: one game pixel per real screen pixel (at most 3 per CSS pixel), so a screen scaled above 100%,
// or a phone, shows finer detail than 1×; on a screen at 100% it's the same as 1×.
const screenDpr = () => Math.min(3, Math.max(1, devicePixelRatio || 1));
function resize() {
  const px = S.pixel === 0 ? 1 / screenDpr() : clamp(Math.round(S.pixel), 1, 6);
  VW = Math.max(80, Math.ceil(innerWidth / px)); VH = Math.max(60, Math.ceil(innerHeight / px));
  renderer.setSize(VW, VH, false);
  canvas.style.width = VW * px + 'px'; canvas.style.height = VH * px + 'px';
  camera.aspect = VW / VH; camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  if (rt) { rt.depthTexture.dispose(); rt.dispose(); }
  rt = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(VW, VH, { minFilter: THREE.NearestFilter, magFilter: THREE.NearestFilter, generateMipmaps: false });
  rt.depthTexture = new THREE.DepthTexture(VW, VH); rt.depthTexture.type = THREE.UnsignedIntType;
  postMat.uniforms.tColor.value = rt.texture; postMat.uniforms.tDepth.value = rt.depthTexture;
  postMat.uniforms.uRes.value.set(VW, VH);
  if (FLAT && SHOWN && !GLIDE) { placeAll(SHOWN); showMarks(); }   // flat pictures are drawn to suit the size
}
function applyLook() {
  postMat.uniforms.uLevels.value = S.levels;
  postMat.uniforms.uDither.value = S.dither ? 1 : 0;
  postMat.uniforms.uOutline.value = S.outline ? 1 : 0;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ textures
function canvasTex(w, h, draw) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h;
  draw(c.getContext('2d'), w, h);
  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
  t.magFilter = t.minFilter = THREE.NearestFilter; t.generateMipmaps = false;
  return t;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ scene: lights + room
// physically based light units (three.js r155+), as in Retro Rack
const PI = Math.PI;
const SQ = 0.057, BORDER = 0.03, B = 8 * SQ + 2 * BORDER, TABLE_Y = -0.02, FLOOR_Y = -0.75;
scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xb7c0ff, 0x2a1a3a, 0.32 * PI));
const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff0d8, 0.17 * PI); sun.position.set(0.6, 3, 1.2); scene.add(sun);
// two low lights from opposite corners, warm and cool, so the upright faces of the pieces differ from one another and
// their shapes show from either side of the board (the lamp and sun are overhead, and light every side face alike)
for (const [x, z, col] of [[1, 0.8, 0xffe2c0], [-1, -0.8, 0xc8d4ff]]) { const f = new THREE.DirectionalLight(col, 0.16 * PI); f.position.set(x, 0.55, z); scene.add(f); }
const lamp = new THREE.PointLight(0xffdca4, 0.75 * PI, 5, 0.43); lamp.position.set(0, 0.7, 0); scene.add(lamp);
// The room (the Room slot, W.room): the colour of the air, the floor and the table, and a few things standing round the
// edge (props). The lights over the board stay as they are in every room, so the pieces always read the same.
const ROOMS = {
  parlour: { bg: '#1a1433', floor: '#2a1d40', table: '#6b3a22' },
  library: { bg: '#17100b', floor: '#3b2515', table: '#1f4a36', leg: '#4a2a16', props: 'shelves' },
  cafe: { bg: '#2b2420', floor: '#ffffff', tiles: ['#b9afa2', '#5a4a44'], table: '#bdb9b0', leg: '#3a3a3e', props: 'cafe' },
  park: { bg: '#8cc0e6', floor: '#4f8a3c', table: '#85857f', props: 'trees' },
  arcade: { bg: '#0a0716', floor: '#ffffff', tiles: ['#141030', '#141030', '#ff4fb4'], table: '#221a44', props: 'cabinets' },
  space: { bg: '#04030a', floor: null, table: '#1a1830', props: 'stars' },
};
const lam = (color, more) => new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color, flatShading: true, ...more });
const floorMat = lam('#2a1d40'), tableMat = lam('#6b3a22', { flatShading: false }), legMat = lam('#6b3a22', { flatShading: false });
const floor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(40, 40), floorMat);   // it reaches past the fog, so its edge never shows
floor.rotation.x = -PI / 2; floor.position.y = FLOOR_Y; scene.add(floor);
const table = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.42, 0.04, 24), tableMat); table.position.y = TABLE_Y - 0.02; scene.add(table);
const leg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.08, TABLE_Y - 0.04 - FLOOR_Y, 8), legMat); leg.position.y = (TABLE_Y - 0.04 + FLOOR_Y) / 2; scene.add(leg);
const PROPS = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(PROPS);
const prop = (geo, mat, x, y, z, ry = 0, to = PROPS) => { const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat); m.position.set(x, y, z); m.rotation.y = ry; to.add(m); return m; };
const boxG = (w, h, d) => new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d), cylG = (r0, r1, h, n = 8) => new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r1, r0, h, n);
// floor tiles: a tiny picture repeated (two colours chequered; a third makes it lines on a ground instead)
const TILES = {};
function tiles(cols) {
  const k = cols.join();
  if (!TILES[k]) {
    const t = canvasTex(8, 8, g => { g.fillStyle = cols[0]; g.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 8); g.fillStyle = cols[2] || cols[1]; if (cols[2]) { g.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 1); g.fillRect(0, 0, 1, 8); } else { g.fillRect(0, 0, 4, 4); g.fillRect(4, 4, 4, 4); } });
    t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.repeat.set(50, 50); TILES[k] = t;
  }
  return TILES[k];
}
const PROPS_OF = {
  shelves(rnd) {   // bookcases in a ring, and a rug under the table
    const wood = lam('#3a2414'), books = ['#8a2f3a', '#2f5a4a', '#c9a24a', '#2f4f7a', '#6a3c7a', '#b5653a'].map(c => lam(c));
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      const a = i / 9 * 2 * PI + 0.35, g = new THREE.Group(); g.position.set(Math.sin(a) * 2.3, FLOOR_Y, Math.cos(a) * 2.3); g.rotation.y = a + PI; PROPS.add(g);
      prop(boxG(1, 1.9, 0.32), wood, 0, 0.95, 0, 0, g);
      for (let row = 0; row < 4; row++) for (let x = -0.44; x < 0.38;) { const w = 0.05 + rnd() * 0.05, h = 0.24 + rnd() * 0.1; prop(boxG(w, h, 0.12), books[Math.floor(rnd() * 6)], x + w / 2, 0.16 + row * 0.44 + h / 2, 0.13, 0, g); x += w + 0.004; }
    }
    prop(new THREE.CircleGeometry(1.15, 20), lam('#6a2030'), 0, FLOOR_Y + 0.003, 0).rotation.x = -PI / 2;
  },
  cafe(rnd) {   // little round tables with stools, and a counter
    const top = lam('#e6e2da'), dark = lam('#3a3a3e'), seat = lam('#b5653a');
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const a = i / 6 * 2 * PI + 0.5, r = 1.9 + rnd() * 0.7, x = Math.sin(a) * r, z = Math.cos(a) * r;
      prop(cylG(0.3, 0.3, 0.03, 10), top, x, FLOOR_Y + 0.7, z); prop(cylG(0.03, 0.03, 0.7, 6), dark, x, FLOOR_Y + 0.35, z);
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const b = a + s * 0.24; prop(cylG(0.13, 0.13, 0.04, 8), seat, Math.sin(b) * (r + 0.1), FLOOR_Y + 0.45, Math.cos(b) * (r + 0.1)); prop(cylG(0.025, 0.025, 0.45, 5), dark, Math.sin(b) * (r + 0.1), FLOOR_Y + 0.225, Math.cos(b) * (r + 0.1)); }
    }
    prop(boxG(2.4, 1, 0.5), lam('#5a3a2a'), 0, FLOOR_Y + 0.5, -3.2); prop(boxG(2.5, 0.05, 0.6), top, 0, FLOOR_Y + 1.02, -3.2); prop(boxG(0.4, 0.45, 0.3), lam('#c4cbd6'), -0.7, FLOOR_Y + 1.27, -3.2);
  },
  trees(rnd) {   // trees and a bench, on grass
    const trunk = lam('#6a4426'), leaf = [lam('#2f6a2c'), lam('#3f8a3a'), lam('#4f9a40')], wood = lam('#8a5a32');
    for (let i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
      const a = i / 11 * 2 * PI + rnd() * 0.3, r = 2.1 + rnd() * 2.6, x = Math.sin(a) * r, z = Math.cos(a) * r, k = 0.8 + rnd() * 0.6;
      prop(cylG(0.09 * k, 0.06 * k, 0.9 * k, 6), trunk, x, FLOOR_Y + 0.45 * k, z);
      prop(cylG(0.52 * k, 0, 1 * k, 7), leaf[i % 3], x, FLOOR_Y + 1.3 * k, z); prop(cylG(0.38 * k, 0, 0.8 * k, 7), leaf[(i + 1) % 3], x, FLOOR_Y + 1.85 * k, z);
    }
    prop(boxG(1.1, 0.05, 0.3), wood, 0, FLOOR_Y + 0.42, 1.25); prop(boxG(1.1, 0.3, 0.05), wood, 0, FLOOR_Y + 0.62, 1.4);
    for (const x of [-0.45, 0.45]) prop(boxG(0.06, 0.42, 0.28), wood, x, FLOOR_Y + 0.21, 1.25);
  },
  cabinets() {   // arcade machines in a ring, their screens lit, and two coloured lights
    const body = lam('#1c1636'), cols = ['#35e0ff', '#ff4fb4', '#ffc56b', '#7dffb0'];
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      const a = i / 8 * 2 * PI + 0.4, g = new THREE.Group(); g.position.set(Math.sin(a) * 2.3, FLOOR_Y, Math.cos(a) * 2.3); g.rotation.y = a + PI; PROPS.add(g);
      const lit = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: cols[i % 4] });
      prop(boxG(0.62, 1.6, 0.6), body, 0, 0.8, 0, 0, g); prop(boxG(0.46, 0.34, 0.02), lit, 0, 1.12, 0.31, 0, g); prop(boxG(0.62, 0.14, 0.04), lit, 0, 1.5, 0.32, 0, g); prop(boxG(0.5, 0.05, 0.22), lam('#3a2f66'), 0, 0.82, 0.38, 0, g);
    }
    for (const [x, z, c] of [[-1.5, 1.1, 0xff4fb4], [1.5, -1.1, 0x35e0ff]]) { const l = new THREE.PointLight(c, 0.5 * PI, 5, 0.6); l.position.set(x, 0.9, z); PROPS.add(l); }
  },
  stars(rnd) {   // stars all round, a planet, and a lit rim on the table, which floats
    const pos = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 700; i++) { const u = rnd() * 2 - 1, a = rnd() * 2 * PI, r = Math.sqrt(1 - u * u) * 9.5; pos.push(Math.cos(a) * r, u * 9.5, Math.sin(a) * r); }
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
    PROPS.add(new THREE.Points(g, new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: '#e8ecff', size: 1.5, sizeAttenuation: false, fog: false, depthWrite: false })));
    prop(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.2, 10, 7), lam('#c9703a', { fog: false }), -4.6, 0.3, -6.2);
    prop(new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.9, 0.09, 3, 24), lam('#e0c890', { fog: false }), -4.6, 0.3, -6.2).rotation.x = 1.25;
    prop(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.42, 0.012, 4, 24), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#6cb8ff' }), 0, TABLE_Y - 0.04, 0).rotation.x = PI / 2;
  },
};
let roomOn = '';
function applyRoom() {
  const r = ROOMS[W.room] || ROOMS.parlour;
  if (roomOn === W.room) return; roomOn = W.room;
  ROOM.set(r.bg); scene.fog.color.set(r.bg);
  floor.visible = r.floor !== null; if (r.floor) floorMat.color.set(r.floor);
  floorMat.map = r.tiles ? tiles(r.tiles) : null; floorMat.needsUpdate = true;
  tableMat.color.set(r.table); legMat.color.set(r.leg || r.table); leg.visible = r.floor !== null;
  PROPS.traverse(o => { if (o.geometry) o.geometry.dispose(); }); PROPS.clear();
  if (r.props) PROPS_OF[r.props](C.rng(7));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ board
// Squares of 5.7 cm with a 3 cm border, on a small round table. Square s (0 = a1 … 63 = h8) is centred at
// (sqX(s), 0, sqZ(s)); White sits at +z, so from White's side a1 is at the near left.
const sqX = s => ((s & 7) - 3.5) * SQ, sqZ = s => (3.5 - (s >> 3)) * SQ;

  tournament: { l: '#b4b38c', d: '#55804a', f: '#2f4a2c', ink: '#c9c8a2' },
  walnut: { l: '#bc9c6c', d: '#704226', f: '#3c2414', ink: '#c9a878' },
  slate: { l: '#969ba6', d: '#525a68', f: '#2c313a', ink: '#a4a9b4' },
  ocean: { l: '#8cb0c0', d: '#2c6690', f: '#173a55', ink: '#a4c8d6' },
  rosewood: { l: '#bea486', d: '#822c38', f: '#4a1620', ink: '#d0b698' },
  mint: { l: '#98bca4', d: '#2c7668', f: '#16423a', ink: '#b0d2bc' },
  sand: { l: '#c2aa74', d: '#9a6a34', f: '#6a4420', ink: '#d6be88' },
  hall: { l: '#b2b0a8', d: '#454b58', f: '#1c1f26', ink: '#cfae6a', tex: 'veins' },
  grid: { l: '#34306a', d: '#17123a', f: '#0b0818', ink: '#35e0ff', tex: 'grid', glow: '#35e0ff' },
  galaxy: { l: '#5a4f9a', d: '#221c50', f: '#0d0a24', ink: '#b8c4ff', tex: 'stars', glow: '#ffffff' },
  lava: { l: '#6a3220', d: '#2a1512', f: '#120807', ink: '#ffb060', tex: 'cracks', glow: '#e04a10', anim: true },

// the patterns: each draws one square (16 px), and, on the glow pass, what shines; both passes must ask rnd for the

const PATTERN = {

  },
  grid(g, x0, y0, light, rnd, glow, b) { g.fillStyle = glow ? '#888' : b.glow; g.globalAlpha = glow ? 1 : 0.5; g.fillRect(x0, y0, 16, 1); g.fillRect(x0, y0, 1, 16); g.globalAlpha = 1; },
  stars(g, x0, y0, light, rnd, glow) { const n = 2 + Math.floor(rnd() * 3); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const x = Math.floor(rnd() * 16), y = Math.floor(rnd() * 16), big = rnd() < 0.3; g.fillStyle = glow ? (big ? '#fff' : '#555') : big ? '#ffffff' : '#b8c4ff'; g.fillRect(x0 + x, y0 + y, 1, 1); } },
  cracks(g, x0, y0, light, rnd, glow) {

'#fff' : '#b03c14'; if (x >= 0 && x < 16 && y >= 0 && y < 16) g.fillRect(x0 + x, y0 + y, 1, 1); const r = rnd(); if (r < 0.4) x++; else if (r < 0.6) x--; else if (r < 0.85) y++; else y--; } }
  },
};

const GLYPH = {
  a: ['.#.', '#.#', '###', '#.#', '#.#'], b: ['##.', '#.#', '##.', '#.#', '##.'], c: ['.##', '#..', '#..', '#..', '.##'], d: ['##.', '#.#', '#.#', '#.#', '##.'],
  e: ['###', '#..', '##.', '#..', '###'], f: ['###', '#..', '##.', '#..', '#..'], g: ['.##', '#..', '#.#', '#.#', '.##'], h: ['#.#', '#.#', '###', '#.#', '#.#'],
  1: ['.#.', '##.', '.#.', '.#.', '###'], 2: ['##.', '..#', '.#.', '#..', '###'], 3: ['##.', '..#', '.#.', '..#', '##.'], 4: ['#.#', '#.#', '###', '..#', '..#'],
  5: ['###', '#..', '##.', '..#', '##.'], 6: ['.##', '#..', '###', '#.#', '###'], 7: ['###', '..#', '.#.', '.#.', '.#.'], 8: ['###', '#.#', '###', '#.#', '###'],
};
const drawBoard = (coords, id = 'standard', glow = false) => canvasTex(144, 144, (g) => {   // 16 px a square, 8 px of border
  const b = BOARDS[id], rnd = C.rng(id.length * 977 + id.charCodeAt(0));
  g.fillStyle = glow ? '#000' : b.f; g.fillRect(0, 0, 144, 144);
  for (let r = 0; r < 8; r++) for (let f = 0; f < 8; f++) {

  }
  if (!coords || glow) return;

  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {

    glyph(String(i + 1), 3, 8 + (7 - i) * 16 + 6, false); glyph(String(i + 1), 138, 8 + (7 - i) * 16 + 5, true);
```
