
## src/shell.html

Page markup and CSS. The build fills in the fonts, favicon and code (__PS2P__, __VT323__, __FAVICON__, __BUNDLE__). (467 lines)

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en-GB">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<script>window.RETRO_SET_RELAY = "";</script>
<!-- Online play: the relay address lives in config.js, next to this file. -->
<script src="config.js"></script>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Retro Set: 3D pixel chess</title>
<meta name="theme-color" content="#1a1433">
<link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="data:image/png;base64,__FAVICON__">
<style>
@font-face{font-family:'Press Start 2P';src:url(data:font/woff2;base64,__PS2P__) format('woff2');font-display:block}
@font-face{font-family:'VT323';src:url(data:font/woff2;base64,__VT323__) format('woff2');font-display:block}
/* Retro Rack's palette, so the two games look like a family */
:root{--night:#1a1433;--plum:#3a2452;--plum2:#4e3270;--lamp:#ffc56b;--chalk:#6cb8ff;--rose:#ff6f8f;--paper:#f7ead2;--ink:#0d0a1c;--wpiece:#efe1c3;--bpiece:#2c2140}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
html,body{margin:0;height:100%;overflow:hidden;background:var(--night);color:var(--paper);font-family:'VT323',ui-monospace,monospace;font-size:22px;line-height:1.15;-webkit-user-select:none;user-select:none;touch-action:none;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent}
[hidden]{display:none!important}
#view{position:fixed;left:0;top:0;display:block;image-rendering:pixelated;image-rendering:crisp-edges}
.panel{background:rgba(22,17,44,.9);border:3px solid var(--lamp);box-shadow:6px 6px 0 var(--ink)}
.btn{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:10px;line-height:1.35;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1);padding:8px 10px;cursor:pointer}
.btn:hover{background:var(--plum2)}
.btn:active{transform:translate(2px,2px)}
.btn[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}
.btn:disabled{opacity:.45;cursor:default;transform:none}
.btn.warn{background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink)}
button:focus-visible{outline:3px dashed var(--chalk);outline-offset:2px}

/* shared by the menus and the panels */
.row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:150px 1fr;gap:6px 14px;align-items:start;margin:0 0 12px}
.lbl{color:var(--lamp);font-size:22px;padding-top:6px}
.seg{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
.note{font-size:20px;opacity:.82;max-width:46ch}
.actions{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:20px}
/* menu: a panel on the left with a stack of screens (one for now), the board in view beside it */
#menu{position:fixed;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-start;padding:3vh 4vw;pointer-events:none;overflow:hidden}
#menuPanel{pointer-events:auto;width:min(560px,94vw);max-height:94vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden;padding:0}
#mStage{display:grid;overflow:auto;min-height:0;padding:16px 20px 14px}
/* tabs: Play and Settings along the top of the menu (#hTabs), and the settings' own tabs (#sTabs), in the menu and in a game */
#hTabs,#sTabs{display:flex;flex:none;border-bottom:3px solid var(--plum2)}
#hTabs{padding:0 12px}
.hTab,.sTab{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;line-height:1;color:var(--paper);opacity:.6;background:none;border:0;border-bottom:4px solid transparent;border-radius:0;margin-bottom:-3px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap}
.hTab{font-size:12px;padding:15px 14px 11px}.sTab{font-size:9px;padding:10px 10px 8px}
.hTab:hover,.sTab:hover{opacity:.9}
.hTab[aria-selected="true"],.sTab[aria-selected="true"]{color:var(--lamp);opacity:1;border-bottom-color:var(--lamp)}
.hTab:focus-visible,.sTab:focus-visible{outline:3px dashed var(--chalk);outline-offset:-5px}
#sPages{display:grid;padding-top:12px}
.sPage{grid-area:1 / 1;min-width:0}.sPage.off{visibility:hidden}
/* cosmetics: the locker (what's worn in each slot, the cases and keys in hand), one slot's looks, and the shop */
#cTabs{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;border-bottom:3px solid var(--plum2)}
#coins{margin-left:auto;padding:0 2px 6px;font-size:22px;color:var(--lamp);white-space:nowrap}#coins::before{content:'\25CF  ';color:#ffd76a}
#cPages{padding-top:10px}
.cHead{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:8px;color:var(--lamp);opacity:.8;margin:8px 0 5px}
#slotGrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(7,minmax(0,1fr));gap:6px}
#itemGrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(8,minmax(0,1fr));gap:6px;min-height:156px;align-content:start}
#shopCases{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,minmax(0,1fr));gap:6px}#shopKeys{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(8,minmax(0,1fr));gap:6px}
.tile{position:relative;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:3px;min-width:0;padding:5px 2px 4px;font:inherit;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1)}
.tile:hover{background:var(--plum2)}.tile[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--plum2);outline:3px solid var(--lamp);outline-offset:-3px}
.tile canvas,.tile img{width:48px;height:45px;image-rendering:pixelated;flex:none}
.tile .tName{font-size:16px;line-height:1;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;max-width:100%}
.tile .tSlot{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:6px;color:var(--lamp);white-space:nowrap}
.tile .tNum{position:absolute;top:1px;right:3px;font-size:15px;line-height:1;color:var(--lamp)}.tile .tSlot~.tNum{top:15px;text-shadow:1px 1px 0 var(--ink)}
.tile.worn::after{content:'\2713';position:absolute;top:0;left:3px;font-size:17px;line-height:1;color:#7dffb0}
#held{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px}
.chip2{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:3px 6px 3px 3px;font:inherit;font-size:18px;line-height:1;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer}
.chip2 canvas{width:24px;height:24px;image-rendering:pixelated}.chip2:hover{background:var(--plum2)}.chip2.none{opacity:.4}
button.chip2:disabled{cursor:default}
.cBar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin:0 0 8px}#slotTitle{font-size:22px;color:var(--lamp)}
.cFoot{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin-top:8px;min-height:34px}
#itemInfo,#shopInfo{font-size:19px;line-height:1.05}
#bEquip,#bBuy,#bOpen{background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink)}#bEquip:disabled,#bBuy:disabled,#bOpen:disabled{background:var(--plum)}
#gift{margin:0;font-size:18px;color:#7dffb0}
#overEarn{font-size:20px;color:#7dffb0;margin:6px 0 0}
#openPanel{width:min(520px,94vw)}
#openKeys .btn[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}
/* trading: the Trade page's rooms, and the trade room (both offers, one inventory at a time, Ready) */
#trRooms{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;max-height:112px;overflow:auto;padding:6px;margin:4px 0 8px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.55);border:3px solid var(--plum2)}
#trRooms .note{margin:2px 4px}#trCode{width:130px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:2px}
#cTrade .onlRow{gap:6px}#bTrCreate{background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink)}
#tradeBox{background:rgba(13,10,28,.82)}#tradePanel{background:#16112c;width:min(780px,96vw);max-height:94vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:7px;padding:12px 16px 12px;overflow:hidden}
#trHead{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px}#trTitle{flex:1;font-size:21px;color:var(--lamp);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
#trSideSeg{flex-wrap:nowrap}#bTrNew{flex:none;font-size:8px;padding:7px 8px}#tradePanel .seg .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 8px}
#trOffers{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px}
.trSide{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:2px 8px;align-items:center;min-width:0;padding:5px 7px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.55);border:3px solid var(--plum2)}
.trSide.ready{border-color:#7dffb0}.trSide .cHead{grid-column:1 / 3;margin:0}
.trStrip{display:flex;gap:4px;min-height:34px;overflow-x:auto;scrollbar-width:thin;align-items:center}
.trStrip:empty::before{content:'Nothing yet';opacity:.5;font-size:18px}
.trStrip .chip2{flex:none;font-size:16px;padding:1px 5px 1px 2px}#trFoot .btn{white-space:nowrap;flex:none}
.trCoins{font-size:18px;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--lamp)}#trCoins{width:74px;font-size:18px;padding:2px 5px}
#trBar{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}
#trGrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(8,minmax(0,1fr));gap:6px;align-content:start;min-height:152px}
#trGrid .note{grid-column:1 / -1}
.tile.inOffer{outline:3px solid #7dffb0;outline-offset:-3px}.tile.marked{outline:3px dashed var(--lamp);outline-offset:-3px}.tile.have{opacity:.5}#trFilter .btn.none{opacity:.45}
#trWait{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;align-items:flex-start;min-height:152px}
#trCodeBig{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:26px;letter-spacing:6px;color:var(--lamp);text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),5px 5px 0 var(--ink)}#trLink{width:100%;font-size:17px}
#trFoot{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;min-height:34px}
#trNote{flex:1;font-size:19px;line-height:1.05;min-width:0}#trNote.count{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:13px;color:#7dffb0}
#trAsks{display:flex;gap:4px}#bTrAdd{background:#7dffb0;color:var(--ink)}
#bTrReady{background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink)}#bTrReady[aria-pressed="true"]{background:#7dffb0}#bTrReady:disabled{background:var(--plum);color:var(--paper)}
/* opening a case fills the whole screen: the reel across the middle, then the prize in its rarity's colour (--rc) */
#reelScreen{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:7;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:2vh;background:rgba(13,10,28,.95);--t:min(40vh,22vw);--rc:#ffc56b;overflow:hidden}
#reelScreen::before{content:'';position:absolute;inset:-40%;background:radial-gradient(closest-side,var(--rc),transparent 72%);opacity:0;transition:opacity .7s;pointer-events:none}
#reelScreen.lit::before{opacity:.3}
#reelScreen.rays::after{content:'';position:absolute;left:50%;top:50%;width:220vmax;height:220vmax;margin:-110vmax 0 0 -110vmax;background:repeating-conic-gradient(var(--rc) 0 5deg,transparent 5deg 20deg);opacity:.13;animation:rays 40s linear infinite;pointer-events:none}
@keyframes rays{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
#rsTitle{position:relative;font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:clamp(11px,2.6vh,20px);color:var(--lamp);text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--ink)}
#reel{position:relative;z-index:1;width:100%;height:calc(var(--t)*1.1 + 6px);flex:none;overflow:hidden;border-top:3px solid var(--plum2);border-bottom:3px solid var(--plum2);background:rgba(13,10,28,.7);cursor:pointer}
#reelStrip{position:absolute;left:0;top:calc(var(--t)*.05);display:flex;gap:6px;will-change:transform}
.reelTile{flex:none;width:var(--t);height:var(--t);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;border:4px solid var(--ink);transition:opacity .45s,transform .45s}
.reelTile canvas{width:78%;height:auto;image-rendering:pixelated}
#reelStrip.done .reelTile{opacity:.25}#reelStrip.done .reelTile.won{opacity:1;transform:scale(1.07);outline:4px solid var(--paper);outline-offset:-2px;z-index:1}
#reelMark{position:absolute;left:50%;top:0;bottom:0;width:4px;margin-left:-2px;background:var(--lamp);box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--ink)}
#reelScreen.lit #reelMark{display:none}
#prizeBox{position:relative;z-index:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:1vh;text-align:center;padding:0 14px}
#prizeName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:clamp(14px,4vh,30px);line-height:1.3;color:var(--rc);text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--ink)}
#prizeNote{font-size:clamp(18px,3.4vh,26px)}
#prizeBtns{margin-top:1vh;justify-content:center}#prizeBtns .btn{font-size:clamp(9px,1.9vh,12px);padding:10px 14px}
#rsHint{font-size:19px;opacity:.6}
#reelScreen:not(.lit) #prizeName,#reelScreen:not(.lit) #prizeNote,#reelScreen:not(.lit) #prizeBtns{visibility:hidden}#reelScreen.lit #rsHint{display:none}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){#reelScreen.rays::after{animation:none}#reelScreen::before,.reelTile{transition:none}}
#setBox:not(.inGame) .sTab[data-tab="match"],#setBox:not(.inGame) .sPage[data-tab="match"]{display:none}
#setBox .row{grid-template-columns:136px 1fr;gap:2px 12px;margin:0 0 9px}#setBox .note{grid-column:2;font-size:18px;margin:0;max-width:none}#setBox .lbl{padding-top:4px}
#setBox .seg .btn{padding:7px 9px}
.mTop{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:14px 20px 0;flex:none}
#mTitle{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:12px;line-height:1.4;color:var(--lamp);margin:0}
/* Vs computer: the bots, the one picked, your colour */
.botGrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,minmax(0,1fr));gap:8px}
.botCard{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:7px 4px 6px;font:inherit;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1)}
.botCard:hover{background:var(--plum2)}
.botCard[aria-pressed="true"]{border-color:var(--lamp);background:var(--plum2)}
.portrait{image-rendering:pixelated;flex:none}
.botName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:9px;color:var(--lamp);white-space:nowrap}
.botElo{font-size:18px;line-height:1}
#botBlurb{font-size:20px;margin:10px 0 8px;min-height:2.3em}
.cpuRow{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:space-between}
.cpuRow .lbl{padding-top:0}
#bPlay,#bStart,#bCreate{font-size:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink)}#bPlay:hover,#bStart:hover,#bCreate:hover{background:#ff8ba5}
#bRejoin{font-size:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}#bRejoin:hover{background:#ffd48e}
/* online (as in Retro Rack): your name, the open rooms, join with a code, create a room; the new room's settings */
input{font-family:'VT323',monospace;font-size:21px;color:var(--paper);background:var(--ink);border:3px solid var(--plum2);border-radius:0;padding:5px 8px;-webkit-user-select:text;user-select:text;outline:none}
input:focus{border-color:var(--chalk)}
.onl{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px}
.field{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-size:20px}
#netName{width:170px}#netCode{width:140px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:2px}
.note.warn{color:var(--rose)}.note.warn:empty{display:none}
.roomsHead{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;font-size:20px;color:var(--lamp);margin-top:4px}
#roomList{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;max-height:172px;overflow:auto;padding:6px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.55);border:3px solid var(--plum2)}
#roomList .note{margin:2px 4px}
.roomItem{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;padding:5px 8px;background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink)}
.roomItem .rn{font-size:20px}.roomItem .rm{font-size:17px;opacity:.8}.roomItem .btn{flex:none}
.onlRow{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between}
#sc-room .row{grid-template-columns:120px 1fr}#sc-room .note{grid-column:2}
/* the waiting room */
#lobbyPanel{width:min(460px,92vw);padding:20px 22px}
#lobbyPanel h2{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:12px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 14px}
#lobbyCode{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:30px;letter-spacing:8px;color:var(--lamp);margin:6px 0 12px;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),5px 5px 0 var(--ink)}
#lobbyStatus{font-size:22px;margin:0 0 10px}
#lobbyLink{width:100%;font-size:17px}
#lobbyPanel .note{margin:12px 0 0}
.mScreen{grid-area:1 / 1;min-width:0}
.title{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(26px,4.2vw,42px);line-height:1.15;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 16px;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),6px 6px 0 var(--plum2),9px 9px 0 var(--ink)}
.quickRow{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px}.quickRow>.btn{margin-bottom:12px;text-align:left}
#bResume{font-size:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}#bResume:hover{background:#ffd48e}
.cards{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:10px}
.card{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;text-align:left;padding:10px 12px;font:inherit;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),4px 4px 0 var(--ink);transition:transform .12s,background .12s,border-color .12s}
.card:hover:not(:disabled){background:var(--plum2);border-color:var(--lamp);transform:translate(-1px,-2px)}
.card:active:not(:disabled){transform:translate(2px,2px)}
.card:disabled{opacity:.5;cursor:default}
.cardName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:11px;line-height:1.3;color:var(--lamp);white-space:nowrap}
.cardTxt{font-size:18px;line-height:1;opacity:.72}
#homeFoot{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;gap:0 14px;margin:12px 0 0}
#ver{margin:0;font-size:16px;opacity:.5}
/* in a game: the players and whose move it is along the top, the moves on the right, the buttons bottom left */
#hud{position:fixed;inset:0;pointer-events:none}
#bar{position:fixed;top:8px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:stretch}
.pl,#mid,#movesBox{background:rgba(22,17,44,.86);border:3px solid var(--plum2);box-shadow:5px 5px 0 var(--ink)}
.pl{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:6px 10px;font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:8px;white-space:nowrap}
.pl.active{border-color:var(--lamp)}
.pl.active::before{content:'\25B6';color:var(--lamp)}
.chip{width:12px;height:12px;border:2px solid var(--ink);flex:none}
#plW .chip{background:var(--wpiece)}#plB .chip{background:var(--bpiece);outline:1px solid var(--paper)}
.plFace:empty{display:none}.plFace img{display:block;height:24px;width:auto;margin:-4px 0}
#toast{position:fixed;top:calc(var(--bb,60px) + 8px);left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);padding:5px 12px;font-size:20px;background:var(--ink);border:3px solid var(--lamp);white-space:nowrap;max-width:94vw}
#mid{min-width:190px;padding:5px 10px;text-align:center}
#net{font-size:16px;margin-top:3px}#net:empty{display:none}
#net::before{content:'\25CF  ';color:#7dffb0}#net[data-ok="0"]::before{color:var(--rose)}
#chatPop{position:fixed;left:8px;bottom:52px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:6px;padding:8px;z-index:6;pointer-events:auto}
#chatPop .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 6px}
.offerPanel p{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:22px}
#turnLine{font-size:20px}
#turnLine.check{color:var(--rose)}
#lastMove{font-size:16px;opacity:.75}
#lastMove:empty{display:none}
#movesBox{position:fixed;top:8px;right:8px;width:164px;max-height:calc(100vh - 80px);display:flex;flex-direction:column;pointer-events:auto}
#movesBox h3{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:8px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0;padding:7px 9px 5px}
#moveList{overflow-y:auto;padding:0 9px 7px;font-size:19px;line-height:1.1;scrollbar-color:var(--plum2) transparent;-webkit-user-select:text;user-select:text}
#moveList:empty::before{content:'No moves yet';opacity:.6}
.mvRow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:28px 1fr 1fr;gap:4px}.mvN{opacity:.55}
.mvRow span.now{color:var(--lamp)}
#tools{position:fixed;left:8px;bottom:8px;display:flex;gap:6px;pointer-events:auto}
#tools .btn{font-size:9px}
/* panels over the game: Menu (pause), the promotion choice and the result */
.overlay{position:fixed;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(13,10,28,.45);padding:4vh 4vw;z-index:5}
.ovPanel{width:min(460px,92vw);max-height:92vh;overflow:auto;padding:20px 22px 18px}
#pausePanel{width:min(560px,94vw);padding:6px 20px 16px}
#confirm{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:52px;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:8px 12px;font-size:22px;white-space:nowrap;pointer-events:auto}
#bConfirm{background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink)}
#rotate{position:fixed;left:50%;top:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);width:min(300px,86vw);padding:14px 16px 16px;text-align:center;z-index:8}
#rotate p{margin:0 0 12px;font-size:22px}
.ovPanel h2{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:16px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 14px}
.ovPanel .actions{margin-top:14px}
#pauseBtns{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px}
#pauseBtns .btn{padding:11px 8px}
#promo{background:rgba(13,10,28,.25)}
#promoBtns{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:8px}
#promoBtns .btn{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:10px 4px 8px}
#promoBtns canvas{width:48px;height:60px;image-rendering:pixelated}
#overTitle{font-size:24px!important;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),6px 6px 0 var(--ink)}
#overWhy{font-size:24px;margin:0 0 4px}
#overMoves{font-size:19px;opacity:.75;margin:0}
/* phones held sideways (and other short screens): smaller everything, so every screen fits without scrolling */
@media (max-height:500px){
  #menu{padding:2vh 3vw}
  .title{font-size:22px;margin-bottom:10px}
  #mStage{padding:10px 14px 8px}
  .cards{grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(190px,1fr));gap:7px}
  .card{padding:6px 9px;gap:4px}.cardName{font-size:9px}.cardTxt{font-size:16px}
  #bResume{font-size:10px;padding:9px 12px}.quickRow>.btn{margin-bottom:8px}
  .note{font-size:18px}
  #homeFoot{margin-top:6px}#ver{font-size:15px}
  .mTop{padding:8px 14px 0}
  .botGrid{gap:5px}.botCard{padding:4px 3px;gap:2px;flex-direction:row;justify-content:center}.botCard .portrait{height:22px;width:auto}.botName{font-size:7px}.botElo{font-size:15px}
  #botBlurb{font-size:16px;margin:5px 0 4px;min-height:0}
  #bPlay{font-size:10px;padding:8px 12px}.cpuRow .btn{font-size:8px;padding:6px 7px}
  #bStart,#bCreate,#bRejoin{font-size:10px;padding:9px 12px}
  .onl{gap:5px}#roomList{max-height:78px}#netName,#netCode{font-size:18px;padding:2px 6px}.field,.roomsHead{font-size:18px}
  #sc-room .row{grid-template-columns:90px 1fr;margin-bottom:6px}#sc-room .lbl{font-size:18px;padding-top:3px}#sc-room .note{font-size:16px}
  #menu .seg .btn{font-size:8px;padding:6px 7px}
  #lobbyPanel{padding:12px 16px}#lobbyCode{font-size:22px;margin:2px 0 6px}#lobbyStatus{font-size:18px;margin-bottom:6px}#lobbyPanel .note{margin-top:6px;font-size:16px}#lobbyPanel .actions{margin-top:8px}
  #chatPop{bottom:44px}
  .ovPanel{padding:12px 16px 12px}.ovPanel h2{font-size:12px;margin-bottom:8px}.ovPanel .row{margin-bottom:6px}
  #pausePanel{padding:2px 14px 10px}.hTab{font-size:10px;padding:10px 12px 7px}.sTab{font-size:8px;padding:8px 9px 6px}#sPages{padding-top:7px}
  #setBox .row{grid-template-columns:118px 1fr;margin-bottom:5px}#setBox .lbl{font-size:19px;padding-top:2px}#setBox .note{font-size:16px}#setBox .seg .btn{font-size:8px;padding:5px 7px}
  #confirm{bottom:44px;font-size:19px;padding:5px 9px}
  #cPages{padding-top:6px}.cHead{margin:5px 0 3px}.tile{padding:3px 2px 2px;gap:1px}.tile canvas,.tile img{width:40px;height:37px}.tile .tName{font-size:14px}
  #itemGrid{min-height:126px}.chip2{font-size:16px;padding:2px 5px 2px 2px}.chip2 canvas{width:20px;height:20px}.cFoot{margin-top:5px;min-height:28px}#itemInfo,#shopInfo{font-size:16px}
  .cBar{margin-bottom:5px}#slotTitle{font-size:18px}#gift{font-size:15px}#coins{font-size:18px;padding-bottom:4px}
  #trRooms{max-height:62px;margin:2px 0 5px}#trCode{font-size:18px;padding:2px 6px}
  #tradePanel{padding:6px 12px 8px;gap:4px}#trTitle{font-size:17px}.trSide{padding:2px 6px}.trStrip{min-height:26px}.trCoins,#trCoins{font-size:16px}
  #trGrid{min-height:120px;gap:4px}#trWait{min-height:120px;gap:3px}#trCodeBig{font-size:18px}#trNote{font-size:16px}#trFoot{min-height:28px}#tradePanel .btn{font-size:8px;padding:5px 7px}
  #reelScreen{gap:1.2vh}#prizeBtns{margin-top:0}#prizeBtns .btn{padding:7px 10px}#rsHint{font-size:16px}
  #overTitle{font-size:16px!important}#overWhy{font-size:20px}
  #promoBtns canvas{width:36px;height:45px}#promoBtns .btn{padding:6px 4px;gap:4px}
}
@media (max-width:760px),(max-height:500px){
  #movesBox{display:none}
  .pl{padding:5px 7px;font-size:7px;gap:6px}.chip{width:10px;height:10px}
  #mid{min-width:130px;padding:3px 7px}#turnLine{font-size:17px}#lastMove{font-size:14px}
  #tools .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 8px}
}
@media (max-width:440px){
  .pl .plName{display:none}#mid{min-width:0}   /* upright phones: the colour chips say who's who */
  .row{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  #trGrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(5,minmax(0,1fr));gap:4px;min-height:0}#trGrid .tile{padding:3px 1px 2px;gap:1px}#trGrid .tile canvas{width:40px;height:37px}#trGrid .tile .tName{font-size:14px}
  #trOffers{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:5px}#trHead{flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;justify-content:space-between}#trTitle{display:none}#tradePanel{padding:10px 12px;gap:6px}.trSide{padding:3px 6px}
  #trFilter{gap:4px}#tradePanel .seg .btn{padding:6px 6px}#trFoot{flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:flex-end;gap:5px 8px}#trNote{flex-basis:100%}
  #slotGrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))}#itemGrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))}#shopCases{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr))}#shopKeys{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))}
  .hTab{font-size:10px;padding:13px 8px 10px}
  #setBox .row{grid-template-columns:1fr}#setBox .note{grid-column:1}.sTab{padding:10px 7px 8px}#pausePanel{padding:6px 12px 14px}
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.card{transition:none}.card:hover:not(:disabled){transform:none}}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<canvas id="view" aria-label="3D chess board"></canvas>
<div id="menu">
  <div id="menuPanel" class="panel">
    <div id="hTabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Main menu"><button class="hTab" type="button" role="tab" data-go="home" aria-selected="true">Play</button><button class="hTab" type="button" role="tab" data-go="cos" aria-selected="false">Cosmetics</button><button class="hTab" type="button" role="tab" data-go="settings" aria-selected="false">Settings</button></div>
    <div class="mTop" id="mTop" hidden><button class="btn" id="mBack" type="button" aria-label="Back">&lt; Back</button><h2 id="mTitle"></h2></div>
    <div id="mStage">
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-home">
        <h1 class="title">Retro Set</h1>
        <div class="quickRow"><button class="btn" id="bRejoin" type="button" hidden></button><button class="btn" id="bResume" type="button" hidden></button></div>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" id="bLocal"><span class="cardName">Two players</span><span class="cardTxt">One device, take turns</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" id="bCpu"><span class="cardName">Vs computer</span><span class="cardTxt">Ten opponents</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" id="bOnline"><span class="cardName">Online</span><span class="cardTxt">Rooms and invite links</span></button>
        </div>
        <div id="homeFoot"><p id="ver"></p><p id="gift" role="status" hidden></p></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-cos" hidden>
        <div id="cTabs"><button class="sTab cTab" type="button" data-c="locker" aria-selected="true">Locker</button><button class="sTab cTab" type="button" data-c="shop" aria-selected="false">Shop</button><button class="sTab cTab" type="button" data-c="trade" aria-selected="false">Trade</button><span id="coins" title="Coins"></span></div>
        <div id="cPages">
          <div class="cPage" id="cLocker">
            <div id="slotGrid"></div>
            <div class="cHead">Unopened</div>
            <div id="held"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="cPage" id="cSlot" hidden>
            <div class="cBar"><button class="btn" id="bSlotBack" type="button">&lt; Locker</button><span id="slotTitle"></span></div>
            <div id="itemGrid"></div>
            <div class="cFoot"><span id="itemInfo"></span><button class="btn" id="bEquip" type="button">Equip</button></div>
          </div>
          <div class="cPage" id="cTrade" hidden>
            <div class="roomsHead"><span>Open trade rooms</span><span id="trLive"></span></div>
            <div id="trRooms"></div>
            <div class="onlRow"><div class="seg"><input id="trCode" maxlength="8" placeholder="Room code" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Trade room code"><button class="btn" id="bTrJoin" type="button">Join with code</button></div>
              <div class="seg" id="trListed"><button class="btn" type="button" data-v="1">Listed</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-v="0">Private</button></div><button class="btn" id="bTrCreate" type="button">Create trade room</button></div>
            <div class="note warn" id="trMenuNote" role="status"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="cPage" id="cShop" hidden>
            <div class="cHead">Cases</div>
            <div id="shopCases"></div>
            <div class="cHead">Keys</div>
            <div id="shopKeys"></div>
            <div class="cFoot"><span id="shopInfo"></span><button class="btn" id="bBuy" type="button">Buy</button></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-settings" hidden><div id="setHome"></div></section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-online" data-title="Online" hidden>
        <div class="onl">
          <label class="field">Your name <input id="netName" maxlength="14" placeholder="Player" autocomplete="nickname"></label>
          <div class="roomsHead"><span>Open rooms</span><span id="roomsLive"></span></div>
          <div id="roomList"></div>
```

## relay/src/index.js

The Cloudflare Worker relay: a Room Durable Object per room code, and one Lobby for the open-rooms list. (182 lines)

```js
// Retro Set relay: a tiny Cloudflare Worker that passes messages between the two players in a room and keeps a live
// list of open rooms. It never plays chess itself; each browser checks every move. It is Retro Rack's relay (without
// its ideas box), so the two games' online play works the same way, but it runs as a Worker of its own.
import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";

// a listed room shows the colour its host chose to play
const MODES = ["random", "white", "black"];
const cleanName = s => String(s || "").replace(/[\u0000-\u001f<>]/g, "").trim().slice(0, 14) || "Player";
const lobbyStub = env => env.LOBBY.get(env.LOBBY.idFromName("lobby"));

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const isWs = request.headers.get("Upgrade") === "websocket";
    if (url.pathname === "/lobby") {
      if (!isWs) return new Response("This address expects a WebSocket connection.", { status: 426 });
      return lobbyStub(env).fetch(request);
    }
    const m = url.pathname.match(/^\/room\/([A-Za-z0-9]{3,12})$/);
    if (!m) {
      return new Response("Retro Set relay is running.", { headers: { "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" } });
    }
    if (!isWs) return new Response("This address expects a WebSocket connection.", { status: 426 });
    return env.ROOMS.get(env.ROOMS.idFromName(m[1].toUpperCase())).fetch(request);
  },
};

// One Durable Object per room. Uses the WebSocket Hibernation API, so an idle room costs nothing.
export class Room extends DurableObject {
  constructor(ctx, env) {
    super(ctx, env);
    this.ctx.setWebSocketAutoResponse(new WebSocketRequestResponsePair("ping", "pong"));
  }

  live(except) {
    return this.ctx.getWebSockets()
      .filter(ws => ws !== except)
      .map(ws => ({ ws, a: ws.deserializeAttachment() || {} }))
      .filter(s => s.a.seat === 0 || s.a.seat === 1);
  }

  async lobby(op, arg) {
    try { await lobbyStub(this.env)[op](arg); } catch (e) { /* the room still works if the list is unavailable */ }
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const code = (url.pathname.split("/").pop() || "").toUpperCase();
    const cid = (url.searchParams.get("cid") || "").replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, "").slice(0, 32) || crypto.randomUUID();
    const { 0: client, 1: server } = new WebSocketPair();
    this.ctx.acceptWebSocket(server);

    let others = this.live(server);
    // the same browser tab reconnecting replaces its old connection and keeps its seat
    let seat = -1;
    for (const s of others) if (s.a.cid === cid) { seat = s.a.seat; s.ws.serializeAttachment({ cid, seat: -1 }); try { s.ws.close(4000, "replaced"); } catch (e) {} }
    others = others.filter(s => s.a.cid !== cid);
    const taken = new Set(others.map(s => s.a.seat));
    const seats = (await this.ctx.storage.get("seats")) || {};
    if (seat < 0) for (const k of [0, 1]) if (seats[k] === cid && !taken.has(k)) seat = k;
    if (seat < 0) for (const k of [0, 1]) if (!taken.has(k)) { seat = k; break; }

    if (seat < 0) {
      server.serializeAttachment({ cid, seat: -1 });
      server.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "full" }));
      server.close(4001, "room full");
      return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
    }
    server.serializeAttachment({ cid, seat });
    seats[seat] = cid;
    await this.ctx.storage.put("seats", seats);
    await this.ctx.storage.deleteAlarm();

    // open-rooms list: a listed room shows while its creator waits, and disappears once a second player joins
    const meta = (await this.ctx.storage.get("meta")) || { code };
    if (others.length > 0) {
      if (!meta.played) { meta.played = true; await this.ctx.storage.put("meta", meta); if (meta.listed) await this.lobby("removeRoom", code); }
    } else if (url.searchParams.get("list") === "1" && !meta.played) {
      const mode = MODES.includes(url.searchParams.get("mode")) ? url.searchParams.get("mode") : "random";
      Object.assign(meta, { listed: true, name: cleanName(url.searchParams.get("name")), mode });
      await this.ctx.storage.put("meta", meta);
      await this.lobby("addRoom", { code, name: meta.name, mode });
    }

    server.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "welcome", seat, peer: others.length > 0 }));
    for (const s of others) { try { s.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "peer", on: true })); } catch (e) {} }
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
  }

  async webSocketMessage(ws, message) {
    if (typeof message !== "string" || message.length > 20000) return;
    const a = ws.deserializeAttachment() || {};
    if (a.seat !== 0 && a.seat !== 1) return;
    let o;
    try { o = JSON.parse(message); } catch (e) { return; }
    if (!o || typeof o.t !== "string") return;
    o.from = a.seat;
    const out = JSON.stringify(o);
    for (const s of this.live(ws)) if (s.a.seat !== a.seat) { try { s.ws.send(out); } catch (e) {} }
  }

  async webSocketClose(ws, code) {
    await this.gone(ws);
    try { ws.close(code === 1005 || code === 1006 ? 1000 : code, "closing"); } catch (e) {}
  }

  async webSocketError(ws) { await this.gone(ws); }

  async gone(ws) {
    const a = ws.deserializeAttachment() || {};
    const rest = this.live(ws);
    if ((a.seat === 0 || a.seat === 1) && !rest.some(s => s.a.seat === a.seat)) {
      for (const s of rest) { try { s.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "peer", on: false })); } catch (e) {} }
    }
    if (!rest.length) {
      const meta = (await this.ctx.storage.get("meta")) || {};
      if (meta.listed && !meta.played) await this.lobby("removeRoom", meta.code);
      await this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(Date.now() + 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    }
  }

  // tidy up rooms nobody has used for six hours
  async alarm() {
    if (!this.ctx.getWebSockets().length) await this.ctx.storage.deleteAll();
  }
}

// A single Durable Object holding the list of open rooms. Menus watch it over a WebSocket and get
// pushed a fresh list whenever a room opens or closes.
export class Lobby extends DurableObject {
  constructor(ctx, env) {
    super(ctx, env);
    this.ctx.setWebSocketAutoResponse(new WebSocketRequestResponsePair("ping", "pong"));
  }

  async rooms() {
    const r = (await this.ctx.storage.get("rooms")) || {};
    const cutoff = Date.now() - 45 * 60 * 1000;          // forget rooms left waiting for 45 minutes
    for (const k of Object.keys(r)) if (r[k].at < cutoff) delete r[k];
    return r;
  }

  view(r) { return Object.values(r).sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at).slice(0, 30); }

  push(r, only) {
    const msg = JSON.stringify({ t: "rooms", rooms: this.view(r), now: Date.now() });
    for (const ws of only ? [only] : this.ctx.getWebSockets()) { try { ws.send(msg); } catch (e) {} }
  }

  async addRoom(e) {
    const r = await this.rooms();
    r[e.code] = { code: e.code, name: cleanName(e.name), mode: MODES.includes(e.mode) ? e.mode : "random", at: Date.now() };
    const keys = Object.keys(r).sort((a, b) => r[b].at - r[a].at);
    for (const k of keys.slice(50)) delete r[k];
    await this.ctx.storage.put("rooms", r);
    this.push(r);
  }

  async removeRoom(code) {
    const r = await this.rooms();
    if (!r[code]) return;
    delete r[code];
    await this.ctx.storage.put("rooms", r);
    this.push(r);
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const { 0: client, 1: server } = new WebSocketPair();
    this.ctx.acceptWebSocket(server);
    this.push(await this.rooms(), server);
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
  }

  async webSocketMessage(ws, message) {
    if (message === "list") this.push(await this.rooms(), ws);
  }

  async webSocketClose(ws, code) {
    try { ws.close(code === 1005 || code === 1006 ? 1000 : code, "closing"); } catch (e) {}
  }
}
```

## relay/wrangler.jsonc

Relay settings: Worker name, Durable Object bindings and migrations. (19 lines)

```jsonc
{
  // Cloudflare settings for the Retro Set relay. The name must match the Worker name in your dashboard.
  "name": "retro-set-relay",
  "main": "src/index.js",
  "compatibility_date": "2025-06-01",
  "durable_objects": {
    "bindings": [
      { "name": "ROOMS", "class_name": "Room" },
      { "name": "LOBBY", "class_name": "Lobby" }
    ]
  },
  // free-plan friendly SQLite-backed Durable Objects (v2 adds the open-rooms list). A new class needs a new tag;
  // never edit these.
  "migrations": [
    { "tag": "v1", "new_sqlite_classes": ["Room"] },
    { "tag": "v2", "new_sqlite_classes": ["Lobby"] }
  ]
}
```

```js
// Retro Set online play settings.
// Paste your Cloudflare relay address between the quotes, for example:
//   window.RETRO_SET_RELAY = "https://retro-set-relay.yourname.workers.dev";
// Leave it empty to play offline only (two players on one device, and the computer).
window.RETRO_SET_RELAY = "https://retro-set-relay.floppr.workers.dev";
```

## sw.js

Service worker for the installable app (network first). (44 lines)

```js
// Retro Set service worker: lets the installed game start without internet.
// Network first, always: when online, every file comes fresh from the site (so updates arrive straight away and,
// once there is online play, both players get the same version); the saved copy is used only when the network fails.
const CACHE = 'retro-set';
const FILES = ['./', 'config.js', 'manifest.webmanifest', 'icons/icon-192.png', 'icons/icon-512.png'];

self.addEventListener('install', e => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE)
    .then(c => c.addAll(FILES.map(f => new Request(f, { cache: 'no-cache' }))))
    .then(() => self.skipWaiting()));
});

self.addEventListener('activate', e => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.keys()
    .then(keys => Promise.all(keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE).map(k => caches.delete(k))))
    .then(() => self.clients.claim()));
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', e => {
  const req = e.request, url = new URL(req.url);
  if (req.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== location.origin) return;
  // the game is one page, so every visit to it shares one saved copy, whatever comes after the ? or #
  const home = new URL('./', self.registration.scope);
  const page = req.mode === 'navigate' && (url.pathname === home.pathname || url.pathname === home.pathname + 'index.html');
  if (req.mode === 'navigate' && !page) return;   // other pages on the site (README and so on) aren't the game
  const key = page ? home.href : req;
  e.respondWith((async () => {
    try {
      // 'no-cache' checks with the site each time (cheap when nothing changed) instead of trusting the browser cache
      let res = await fetch(page ? req.url : req, { cache: 'no-cache', credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (page && res.redirected) res = await fetch(req);
      if (res.ok && res.type === 'basic') {
        const copy = res.clone();
        e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(key, copy)));
      }
      return res;
    } catch (err) {
      const saved = await caches.match(key, { ignoreSearch: true });
      if (saved) return saved;
      throw err;
    }
  })());
});
```
