## tools/build.js

Builds index.html and these context packs. (40 lines)

```js
// Builds index.html from src/ and vendor/. Run with:  node tools/build.js   (no installs needed)
// Output is one self-contained file: fonts and the three.js engine are inlined so it also works offline.
// It then remakes CONTEXT.md and CONTEXT-SHORT.md, the one-file summaries of the project for AI assistants.
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path'), crypto = require('crypto');
const root = path.join(__dirname, '..');
const read = p => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, p), 'utf8');
const b64 = p => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, p)).toString('base64');

// the game code, in this order; a new file in src/ goes in this list (and in SOURCE in make-context.js)
const CODE = ['src/core.js', 'src/bots.js', 'src/looks.js', 'src/sets.js', 'src/game.js'];

let html = read('src/shell.html');
html = html.replace('__PS2P__', () => b64('vendor/press-start-2p-latin-400-normal.woff2'));
html = html.replace('__VT323__', () => b64('vendor/vt323-latin-400-normal.woff2'));
html = html.replace('__FAVICON__', () => b64('icons/favicon-32.png'));   // inlined, so a downloaded copy has it too

// three.js ES module build: turn its single trailing `export {...}` into a returned namespace object,
// so it can live inside one inline <script type="module"> with the game code.
let three = read('vendor/three.module.min.js').trimEnd();
const m = three.match(/export\s*\{([^}]*)\}\s*;?\s*$/);
if (!m || (three.match(/export/g) || []).length !== 1) throw new Error('Unexpected three.js module format');
const pairs = m[1].split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).map(part => {
  const [loc, pub] = part.split(/\s+as\s+/);
  return `${(pub || loc).trim()}:${loc.trim()}`;
});
three = three.slice(0, m.index) + 'return Object.freeze({' + pairs.join(',') + '});';

let bundle = 'const THREE = (() => {\n' + three + '\n})();\n' + CODE.map(read).join('\n');
if (bundle.includes('</script')) throw new Error('Bundle contains </script, which would break the page');
// The version is a fingerprint of the game code: any code change gives a new one, an unchanged rebuild the same.
// It shows at the bottom of the menu, so the owner can check an update has arrived (and, once there is online
// play, the two players can compare versions before a game starts, because both must run identical code).
const version = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bundle).digest('hex').slice(0, 8);
if (bundle.split("'__BUILD__'").length !== 2) throw new Error("Expected exactly one '__BUILD__' in the game code");
bundle = bundle.replace("'__BUILD__'", `'${version}'`);
html = html.replace('__BUNDLE__', () => bundle);   // function form: the bundle contains $ characters
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'index.html'), html);
console.log(`Built index.html (${Math.round(html.length / 1024)} KB, ${pairs.length} three.js exports, version ${version})`);
console.log('Wrote ' + require('./make-context.js').write());
```

## tools/make-context.js

Builds CONTEXT.md and CONTEXT-SHORT.md (this pack). (130 lines)

~~~~js
// Builds the two "context packs" for AI assistants that can't open the repository (a chat in a browser, say):
//   CONTEXT.md        the project notes, a map of the code, and the full source of everything written for the project
//   CONTEXT-SHORT.md  the notes and the map only, for tools that can't take a file the size of CONTEXT.md
// tools/build.js runs this after every build, and tests/core.test.js fails if either file is out of date.
// Run alone with:  node tools/make-context.js
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path');
const root = path.join(__dirname, '..');
const read = p => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, p), 'utf8');

const DOCS = ['AGENTS.md', 'HANDOVER.md', 'README.md'];
// the source written for this project, in reading order: [path, code-block language, what it is]
const SOURCE = [
  ['src/core.js', 'js', 'The chess rules and the computer player\'s engine. No DOM; also runs in Node for the tests and tools.'],
  ['src/bots.js', 'js', 'The computer opponents: names, faces, blurbs, ratings and settings. No DOM; also runs in Node.'],
  ['src/looks.js', 'js', 'The cosmetics: the catalogue of looks, the cases and keys, what a game and a day pay, and the saved locker. No DOM; also runs in Node.'],
  ['src/sets.js', 'js', 'The piece sets (the shapes of the pieces, built from simple solids) and the finishes (what they are made of). Needs three.js, so browser only.'],
  ['src/game.js', 'js', 'Everything in the browser: rendering, camera, menus and the installable app.'],
  ['src/shell.html', 'html', 'Page markup and CSS. The build fills in the fonts, favicon and code (__PS2P__, __VT323__, __FAVICON__, __BUNDLE__).'],
  ['relay/src/index.js', 'js', 'The Cloudflare Worker relay: a Room Durable Object per room code, and one Lobby for the open-rooms list.'],
  ['relay/wrangler.jsonc', 'jsonc', 'Relay settings: Worker name, Durable Object bindings and migrations.'],
  ['relay/package.json', 'json', 'The relay\'s packages (Wrangler).'],
  ['config.js', 'js', 'The live relay address.'],
  ['sw.js', 'js', 'Service worker for the installable app (network first).'],
  ['manifest.webmanifest', 'json', 'App name, colours and icons.'],
  ['package.json', 'json', 'npm shortcuts for the commands.'],
  ['tools/build.js', 'js', 'Builds index.html and these context packs.'],
  ['tools/make-context.js', 'js', 'Builds CONTEXT.md and CONTEXT-SHORT.md (this pack).'],
  ['tools/make-icons.js', 'js', 'Draws the app icons.'],
  ['tools/sim-bots.js', 'js', 'Measures and tunes the computer opponents\' strength against Stockfish and each other.'],
  ['tests/core.test.js', 'js', 'Tests of src/core.js; also checks these packs are up to date.'],
  ['tests/browser/lib.js', 'js', 'Shared helpers for the browser tests.'],
  ['tests/browser/run-all.js', 'js', 'Runs the browser tests against a local copy of the site.'],
];
const SKIP_DIRS = new Set(['.git', 'node_modules', '.wrangler']);

function files(dir = '') {
  const out = [];
  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(path.join(root, dir), { withFileTypes: true }).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {
    if (SKIP_DIRS.has(e.name)) continue;
    const p = dir ? dir + '/' + e.name : e.name;
    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...files(p)); else out.push(p);
  }
  return out;
}
const version = () => (read('index.html').match(/const BUILD = '([0-9a-f]{8})'/) || [, 'unknown'])[1];
const firstComment = p => { const m = read(p).match(/^\/\/ (.*)/); return m ? m[1] : ''; };

// a map of a JavaScript file: its "// ----- name" sections with line ranges, and the top-level names in each
function jsMap(p) {
  const lines = read(p).split('\n'), secs = [{ name: '(top of file)', from: 1, names: [] }];
  lines.forEach((l, i) => {
    let m;
    if ((m = l.match(/^\/\/ -{10,} (.+)$/))) secs.push({ name: m[1], from: i + 1, names: [] });
    else if ((m = l.match(/^(?:export )?(?:async )?function\*? ?(\w+)/))) secs[secs.length - 1].names.push(m[1] + '()');
    else if ((m = l.match(/^(?:export )?class (\w+)/))) secs[secs.length - 1].names.push('class ' + m[1]);
    else if ((m = l.match(/^ {2}(?:async )?(\w+)\([^)]*\) \{/)) && m[1] !== 'if' && m[1] !== 'for') secs[secs.length - 1].names.push('.' + m[1] + '()');
    else if ((m = l.match(/^(?:export )?(?:const|let|var) (\w+)/))) secs[secs.length - 1].names.push(m[1]);
    else if ((m = l.match(/^(?:const|let|var) \{ ([\w, ]+) \}/))) secs[secs.length - 1].names.push(...m[1].split(', '));
  });
  secs.forEach((s, i) => { s.to = i + 1 < secs.length ? secs[i + 1].from - 1 : lines.length; });
  return secs.filter(s => s.names.length || s.name !== '(top of file)')
    .map(s => `- **${s.name}** (lines ${s.from}–${s.to}): ${s.names.length ? s.names.map(n => '`' + n + '`').join(', ') : '(no top-level names)'}`).join('\n');
}
function htmlMap(p) {
  const t = read(p), ids = [...t.matchAll(/id="([^"]+)"/g)].map(m => '`#' + m[1] + '`');
  return `Elements with an id, in page order: ${ids.join(', ')}`;
}

function codeMap() {
  const out = ['## Map of the code', '',
    'Made automatically from the source, so it always matches it. Line numbers refer to the files as they are in this version.', ''];
  for (const [p] of SOURCE) {
    if (!/\.(js|html)$/.test(p) || p.startsWith('tools/') || p.startsWith('tests/') || /^(config|sw)\.js$/.test(p)) continue;
    out.push(`### ${p} (${read(p).split('\n').length} lines)`, '', p.endsWith('.html') ? htmlMap(p) : jsMap(p), '');
  }
  out.push('### Browser tests (tests/browser/, not included here)', '',
    'Run with `node tests/browser/run-all.js` (see AGENTS.md, Testing). Each file is one test script:', '');
  for (const f of files('tests/browser').filter(f => f.endsWith('.test.js'))) out.push(`- \`${f}\`: ${firstComment(f)}`);
  out.push('');
  return out.join('\n');
}
function note(f, full) {
  if (SOURCE.some(x => x[0] === f)) return full ? 'included below' : 'included in CONTEXT.md';
  if (DOCS.includes(f)) return 'included below';
  if (/^CONTEXT/.test(f)) return 'a context pack (this file or its partner)';
  if (f.startsWith('tests/browser/')) return 'browser test, listed in the map';
  return { 'index.html': 'built output, left out (generated from src/)', 'CLAUDE.md': 'points Claude Code to AGENTS.md', '.nojekyll': 'stops GitHub Pages running Jekyll',
    'relay/package-lock.json': 'exact package versions for the relay, left out' }[f] || '';
}
function tree(full) {
  const out = ['## Files in the repository', ''], dirs = { vendor: 'three.js r159 and two pixel fonts with their licences; third-party, left out', icons: 'the app icons; images, left out' };
  const seen = new Set(), packs = ['CONTEXT.md', 'CONTEXT-SHORT.md'];   // listed whether or not they exist yet, so the output never depends on it
  const all = [...new Set([...files(), ...packs])].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
  for (const f of all) {
    const d = f.split('/')[0];
    if (dirs[d]) { if (!seen.has(d)) { seen.add(d); out.push(`- \`${d}/\` (${files(d).length} files): ${dirs[d]}`); } continue; }
    const n = note(f, full);
    out.push(`- \`${f}\`${n ? ': ' + n : ''}`);
  }
  out.push('');
  return out.join('\n');
}
const fence = (lang, text) => { const f = text.includes('```') ? '~~~~' : '```'; return `${f}${lang}\n${text.replace(/\n$/, '')}\n${f}`; };

function make() {
  const v = version();
  const head = full => [
    `# Retro Set: ${full ? 'full context' : 'short context'} for an AI assistant`, '',
    `Generated by \`tools/build.js\` from game version **${v}**. Do not edit by hand: change the files it is made from and rebuild.`, '',
    full
      ? 'This one file holds what an AI needs to understand and change Retro Set: the project notes, a map of the code, and the full source of everything written for the project. Left out: the three.js engine and the pixel fonts in `vendor/` (third-party, unchanged), the built `index.html` (generated from `src/`), images, and the browser test scripts (listed in the map).'
      : 'This file holds the project notes and a map of the code, without the source. `CONTEXT.md` is the same plus the full source; use it when the AI can take a file of that size.',
    '', 'Live game: https://flopp-r.github.io/retro-set/ · Repository: https://github.com/flopp-r/retro-set', '',
    'Read AGENTS.md first: it explains how the project works and the rules any change must keep.', '',
  ].join('\n');
  const docs = DOCS.map(d => `---\n\n# File: ${d}\n\n${read(d).replace(/\n$/, '')}\n`).join('\n');
  const src = SOURCE.map(([p, lang, what]) => `## ${p}\n\n${what} (${read(p).split('\n').length} lines)\n\n${fence(lang, read(p))}\n`).join('\n');
  const full = [head(true), tree(true), docs, '---\n', codeMap(), '---\n\n# Source code\n', src].join('\n');
  const short = [head(false), tree(false), docs, '---\n', codeMap()].join('\n');
  return { 'CONTEXT.md': full, 'CONTEXT-SHORT.md': short };
}
function write() {
  const out = make();
  for (const [f, text] of Object.entries(out)) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, f), text);
  return Object.entries(out).map(([f, t]) => `${f} ${Math.round(t.length / 1024)} KB`).join(', ');
}

module.exports = { make, write };
if (require.main === module) console.log('Wrote ' + write());
~~~~

## tools/make-icons.js

Draws the app icons. (78 lines)

```js
// Draws the app icons as pixel art and writes them to icons/. Run with:  node tools/make-icons.js   (no installs needed)
// One 32 x 32 picture (a cream pawn on a plum chequerboard) is scaled up with hard pixel edges. The pawn stays inside
// the middle 80% so phones can crop the icon to a circle or rounded square ("maskable") without cutting it.
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path'), zlib = require('zlib');
const out = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'icons');

const hex = h => [parseInt(h.slice(1, 3), 16), parseInt(h.slice(3, 5), 16), parseInt(h.slice(5, 7), 16)];
const COL = {
  light: hex('#4e3270'), lightDark: hex('#3e2760'), dark: hex('#2c1c44'), darkDark: hex('#221636'), shadow: hex('#170f26'), ink: hex('#0d0a1c'),
  piece: hex('#f7ead2'), pieceLit: hex('#fff4dd'), pieceDark: hex('#d9c7a8'), pieceShade: hex('#b09a7a'),
};
const BAYER = [[0, 8, 2, 10], [12, 4, 14, 6], [3, 11, 1, 9], [15, 7, 13, 5]];
const dither = (x, y, t) => t > (BAYER[y & 3][x & 3] + 0.5) / 16;   // true on roughly a fraction t of pixels

const N = 32, CX = 16;
// the pawn's half-width on a row (grid pixels), or 0 where the row isn't part of it; py is the row's middle
function half(px, py) {
  const head = 4.3, hy = 9.2;
  if (Math.abs(py - hy) <= head) { const w = Math.sqrt(head * head - (py - hy) ** 2); if (Math.abs(px - CX) <= w) return w; }
  if (py >= 13.5 && py < 15.3) return 4.6;                                  // collar
  if (py >= 15.3 && py < 21.5) return 2.4 + 2.8 * ((py - 15.3) / 6.2) ** 1.6;   // body, flaring out
  if (py >= 21.5 && py < 23) return 6;                                      // lip of the base
  if (py >= 23 && py < 25.5) return 7;                                      // base
  return 0;
}
const inside = (x, y) => { const w = half(x + 0.5, y + 0.5); return w > 0 && Math.abs(x + 0.5 - CX) <= w; };

function pixel(x, y) {
  const px = x + 0.5, py = y + 0.5;
  if (inside(x, y)) {
    // lit from the top left: the head as a ball, the rest as a turned shape lit across each row
    let light;
    if (py < 13.5) light = (-(px - CX) - (py - 9.2)) / (1.414 * 4.3);
    else light = -(px - CX) / half(px, py) * 0.9 - (py > 23 ? 0.15 : 0);
    if (Math.hypot(px - 14.4, py - 7.6) < 1.2) return COL.pieceLit;
    if (light > 0.45) return dither(x, y, (light - 0.45) * 3) ? COL.pieceLit : COL.piece;
    if (light < -0.6) return dither(x, y, (-0.6 - light) * 4) ? COL.pieceShade : COL.pieceDark;
    if (light < -0.2) return dither(x, y, (-0.2 - light) * 3) ? COL.pieceDark : COL.piece;
    return COL.piece;
  }
  for (const [dx, dy] of [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]]) if (inside(x + dx, y + dy)) return COL.ink;   // the game's dark outline
  if (inside(x - 1, y - 1) || inside(x - 2, y - 1) || (inside(x - 2, y - 2) && y > 20)) return COL.shadow;   // a shadow to the lower right
  const sq = ((x >> 3) + (y >> 3)) & 1;                                     // 8 x 8 squares
  const edge = Math.hypot(px - 16, py - 16) / 22.6;                          // darker towards the corners, dithered like the game
  const dk = dither(x, y, (edge - 0.45) * 1.8);
  return sq ? (dk ? COL.darkDark : COL.dark) : (dk ? COL.lightDark : COL.light);
}

const CRC = new Int32Array(256).map((_, n) => { let c = n; for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1; return c; });
const crc32 = buf => { let c = -1; for (const b of buf) c = CRC[(c ^ b) & 255] ^ (c >>> 8); return (c ^ -1) >>> 0; };
function chunk(type, data) {
  const len = Buffer.alloc(4); len.writeUInt32BE(data.length);
  const td = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(type, 'ascii'), data]);
  const crc = Buffer.alloc(4); crc.writeUInt32BE(crc32(td));
  return Buffer.concat([len, td, crc]);
}
function png(size) {
  const raw = Buffer.alloc(size * (size * 3 + 1));
  for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
    raw[y * (size * 3 + 1)] = 0;                     // filter: none
    for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
      const c = pixel(Math.floor(x * N / size), Math.floor(y * N / size));
      raw.set(c, y * (size * 3 + 1) + 1 + x * 3);
    }
  }
  const ihdr = Buffer.alloc(13);
  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(size, 0); ihdr.writeUInt32BE(size, 4);
  ihdr[8] = 8; ihdr[9] = 2;                          // 8 bits per channel, RGB
  return Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]), chunk('IHDR', ihdr),
    chunk('IDAT', zlib.deflateSync(raw, { level: 9 })), chunk('IEND', Buffer.alloc(0))]);
}

fs.mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });
for (const [name, size] of [['favicon-32.png', 32], ['icon-192.png', 192], ['icon-512.png', 512]]) {
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, name), png(size));
  console.log(`Wrote icons/${name} (${size} x ${size})`);
}
```

## tools/sim-bots.js

Measures and tunes the computer opponents' strength against Stockfish and each other. (175 lines)

```js
// Measures the computer opponents' strength by playing games with the real rules and engine: against Stockfish at its
// calibrated strength settings (the anchor), and against each other (the 200-point gaps). Usage:
//   node tools/sim-bots.js match <a> <b> [games]   a, b: a bot (pip, mabel …), a bot with another knob setting
//                                                  (nell@0.15, or nell@0.15/4 to look 4 moves ahead), or Stockfish
//                                                  at a strength (sf1320, sf1500)
//   node tools/sim-bots.js tune [games] [bot]      finds each bot's knob: Nell and Tariq against Stockfish, then each
//                                                  of the others 200 points below the bot above it; prints the values
//                                                  to put into src/bots.js. Given a bot, it starts there, the bots
//                                                  above keeping their values in src/bots.js
//   node tools/sim-bots.js check [games] [bot]     plays each bot against its anchor or the bot above, and prints the
//                                                  measured ratings (given a bot, from there down)
// Stockfish (stockfish.js 19, GPL-3.0) is installed into a temporary folder on first use. It is only used here to
// measure; it is never part of the game. Its strength setting (UCI_Elo, 1320 at the lowest) is calibrated by its
// makers against other engines' ratings, not against people. Games run on every core, in worker threads.
const { Worker, isMainThread, parentPort, workerData } = require('worker_threads');
const path = require('path'), os = require('os'), fs = require('fs');
const C = require('../src/core.js'), BOTS = require('../src/bots.js');

const SF_VERSION = '19.0.1', SF_MS = 60;   // Stockfish's time a move: its weakened play picks its move after a very shallow search, so more time changes little
const MAX_PLY = 240;                       // a game still going after this is decided on material (see adjudicate)

// a player spec: { bot, cfg } or { sf: elo }
function spec(name) {
  const m = /^sf(\d+)$/.exec(name);
  if (m) return { name, sf: +m[1] };
  const [id, rest] = name.split('@'), [k, d] = (rest || '').split('/'), bot = BOTS.byId(id);   // nell@0.1/4: knob 0.1, 4 moves deep
  if (!bot) throw new Error(`No bot called "${id}" (the bots: ${BOTS.LIST.map(b => b.id).join(', ')}; or sf1320 and so on)`);
  const cfg = BOTS.settings(bot, k ? +k : undefined);
  if (d) cfg.depth = +d;
  return { name, bot: id, cfg };
}
const material = b => b.reduce((a, p) => a + (p ? (p === p.toUpperCase() ? 1 : -1) * C.VALUE[C.kind(p)] : 0), 0);
// a game that runs on: decided for the side a clear piece ahead (it would win against an opponent of this strength), else drawn
const adjudicate = g => { const d = material(g.pos.b); return d >= 300 ? 1 : d <= -300 ? 0 : 0.5; };
const elo = s => s <= 0 ? -999 : s >= 1 ? 999 : -400 * Math.log10(1 / s - 1);
const expected = d => 1 / (1 + Math.pow(10, -d / 400));

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ a worker: plays games
if (!isMainThread) {
  const { spawn } = require('child_process');
  let sf = null, buf = '', waiters = [];
  const send = s => sf.stdin.write(s + '\n'), wait = re => new Promise(res => waiters.push({ re, res }));
  async function engine() {
    if (sf) return;
    sf = spawn(process.execPath, [workerData.sfPath], { stdio: 'pipe' });
    sf.stdout.on('data', d => { buf += d; let i; while ((i = buf.indexOf('\n')) >= 0) { const l = buf.slice(0, i).trim(); buf = buf.slice(i + 1); for (const w of waiters.slice()) if (w.re.test(l)) { waiters.splice(waiters.indexOf(w), 1); w.res(l); } } });
    send('uci'); await wait(/^uciok/);
    send('setoption name UCI_LimitStrength value true');
  }
  async function sfMove(g, elo) {
    send(`setoption name UCI_Elo value ${elo}`);
    send(`position fen ${g.start} moves ${g.list.map(x => C.uci(x.m)).join(' ')}`);
    send(`go movetime ${SF_MS}`);
    return (await wait(/^bestmove/)).split(' ')[1];
  }
  async function playGame({ white, black, seed }) {
    let g = C.newGame(), lopsided = 0;
    const rnd = C.rng(seed);
    if (white.sf || black.sf) { await engine(); send('ucinewgame'); send('isready'); await wait(/^readyok/); }
    while (!g.result && g.list.length < MAX_PLY) {
      const p = g.pos.turn === 'w' ? white : black;
      const n = p.sf ? C.playUci(g, await sfMove(g, p.sf)) : C.play(g, C.runThink(g, p.cfg, rnd));
      if (!n) throw new Error('an illegal move from ' + p.name);
      g = n;
      // a side three minor pieces' worth ahead for 6 moves in a row has won, at these strengths: stop early to save time
      lopsided = Math.abs(material(g.pos.b)) >= 700 && g.list.length > 30 ? lopsided + 1 : 0;
      if (lopsided >= 6) break;
    }
    const score = g.result ? (g.result.score === '1-0' ? 1 : g.result.score === '0-1' ? 0 : 0.5) : adjudicate(g);
    return { score, plies: g.list.length, why: g.result ? g.result.why : 'adjudicated' };
  }
  parentPort.on('message', async job => { if (job === 'stop') { if (sf) sf.kill(); process.exit(0); } parentPort.postMessage({ id: job.id, ...(await playGame(job)) }); });
  return;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ the main thread
function ensureStockfish() {
  const dir = path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'retro-set-stockfish'), mod = path.join(dir, 'node_modules', 'stockfish');
  if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(mod, 'package.json'))) {
    console.log(`Installing Stockfish ${SF_VERSION} into ${dir} (first run only)…`);
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    require('child_process').execFileSync('npm', ['install', '--no-save', '--no-audit', '--no-fund', '--prefix', dir, `stockfish@${SF_VERSION}`], { stdio: 'inherit' });
  }
  return require(mod).findEngine('single');
}
let pool = null;
function startPool() {
  const sfPath = ensureStockfish(), n = Math.max(1, os.cpus().length);
  const workers = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Worker(__filename, { workerData: { sfPath } }));
  return { workers, stop: () => workers.forEach(w => w.postMessage('stop')) };
}
// plays `games` games between a and b, half with each colour; returns a's score (0 to 1) and the numbers behind it
function match(a, b, games) {
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const jobs = Array.from({ length: games }, (_, i) => ({ id: i, white: i % 2 ? b : a, black: i % 2 ? a : b, seed: 1000003 * (i + 1) + games }));
    let next = 0, done = 0, pts = 0, w = 0, l = 0, d = 0, plies = 0;
    const why = {};
    const feed = wk => { if (next < jobs.length) wk.postMessage(jobs[next++]); };
    const onMsg = r => {
      const aWhite = r.id % 2 === 0, s = aWhite ? r.score : 1 - r.score;
      pts += s; plies += r.plies; why[r.why] = (why[r.why] || 0) + 1;
      if (s === 1) w++; else if (s === 0) l++; else d++;
      if (++done === games) { for (const wk of pool.workers) wk.off('message', wk._h); resolve({ score: pts / games, w, l, d, plies: plies / games, why }); }
    };
    for (const wk of pool.workers) { wk._h = r => { onMsg(r); feed(wk); }; wk.on('message', wk._h); feed(wk); }
  });
}
// the uncertainty of a score: about 2 standard errors, in rating points
function band(r, games) {
  const v = (r.w * Math.pow(1 - r.score, 2) + r.l * Math.pow(r.score, 2) + r.d * Math.pow(0.5 - r.score, 2)) / games, se = Math.sqrt(v / games);
  return Math.round((elo(Math.min(0.999, r.score + 2 * se)) - elo(Math.max(0.001, r.score - 2 * se))) / 2);
}
const line = (a, b, r, games) => `${a.name.padEnd(12)} v ${b.name.padEnd(12)} ${(r.score * 100).toFixed(1).padStart(5)}%  (${r.w} won, ${r.d} drawn, ${r.l} lost; ${Math.round(r.plies)} plies a game)  ${elo(r.score) >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${Math.round(elo(r.score))} ±${band(r, games)}`;

// each bot's reference and its rating gap to it: the top two against Stockfish, each of the rest against the bot above
const CHAIN = BOTS.LIST.slice().reverse().map((b, i, all) => i === 0 ? [b.id, 'sf1500', b.rating - 1500] : i === 1 ? [b.id, 'sf1320', b.rating - 1320] : [b.id, all[i - 1].id, b.rating - all[i - 1].rating]);

(async () => {
  const [cmd, x, y, z] = process.argv.slice(2);
  if (!['match', 'tune', 'check'].includes(cmd)) { console.log(fs.readFileSync(__filename, 'utf8').split('\n').slice(0, 10).join('\n')); return; }
  pool = startPool();
  const t0 = Date.now();
  try {
    if (cmd === 'match') {
      const a = spec(x), b = spec(y), games = +z || 100;
      console.log(line(a, b, await match(a, b, games), games));
    }
    if (cmd === 'tune') {
      // each bot's knob, top down (so each reference already has its tuned value): k = 0 first (its best), then
      // stepping the knob up until it's below the target, then closing in by straight-line steps in rating points
      // tune 80 mabel: start the chain at Mabel, the bots above keeping their values in src/bots.js
      const games = +x || 80, tuned = Object.fromEntries(BOTS.LIST.map(b => [b.id, b.cfg.k])), E = s => elo(Math.min(0.98, Math.max(0.02, s)));
      const from = y ? CHAIN.findIndex(c => c[0] === y) : 0;
      if (from < 0) throw new Error(`No bot called "${y}" in the chain`);
      for (const [id, ref, gap] of CHAIN.slice(from)) {
        const bot = BOTS.byId(id), want = E(expected(gap));
        const refSpec = ref.startsWith('sf') ? spec(ref) : { name: ref, bot: ref, cfg: BOTS.settings(BOTS.byId(ref), tuned[ref]) };
        const at = async k => {
          const me = { name: `${id}@${k.toFixed(3)}`, cfg: BOTS.settings(bot, k) }, r = await match(me, refSpec, games);
          console.log('  ' + line(me, refSpec, r, games)); return E(r.score);
        };
        // start from the bot's current value, step until the target is between two tries, then close in
        let lo = null, hi = null, k0 = tuned[id], e0 = await at(k0);
        if (e0 >= want) { lo = [k0, e0]; for (let k = k0 + 0.03; k <= 1.6; k += 0.03) { const e = await at(k); if (e < want) { hi = [k, e]; break; } lo = [k, e]; } }
        else { hi = [k0, e0]; for (let k = Math.max(0, k0 - 0.03); ; k = Math.max(0, k - 0.03)) { const e = await at(k); if (e >= want) { lo = [k, e]; break; } hi = [k, e]; if (k === 0) break; } }
        if (!lo) { console.log(`${bot.name} can't reach ${bot.rating} at depth ${bot.cfg.depth}: give it a deeper search or fewer blunders\n`); tuned[id] = 0; continue; }
        if (!hi) { console.log(`${bot.name} is still too strong with the knob at its top\n`); tuned[id] = lo[0]; continue; }
        let k = lo[0];
        for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
          k = lo[0] + (want - lo[1]) * (hi[0] - lo[0]) / (hi[1] - lo[1]);
          k = Math.min(hi[0] - 0.01, Math.max(lo[0] + 0.01, k));
          const e = await at(k);
          if (e >= want) lo = [k, e]; else hi = [k, e];
        }
        tuned[id] = Math.round((lo[0] + (want - lo[1]) * (hi[0] - lo[0]) / (hi[1] - lo[1])) * 1000) / 1000;
        console.log(`${bot.name} (${bot.rating}): k = ${tuned[id]}  (want ${(expected(gap) * 100).toFixed(1)}% against ${ref})\n`);
      }
      console.log('Put these into src/bots.js:'); for (const [id] of CHAIN.slice(from)) console.log(`  ${id}: k ${tuned[id]}`);
    }
    if (cmd === 'check') {
      // each bot against its reference, top down; its rating builds on the reference's measured rating (not its target),
      // so the margins add up down the chain (as the square root of the sum of their squares)
      // check 240 tariq: start at Tariq; a reference not measured in this run counts at its target rating
      const games = +x || 200, got = {}, err = {}, from = y ? CHAIN.findIndex(c => c[0] === y) : 0;
      if (from < 0) throw new Error(`No bot called "${y}" in the chain`);
      for (const [id, ref, gap] of CHAIN.slice(from)) {
        const a = spec(id), b = spec(ref), r = await match(a, b, games), bot = BOTS.byId(id), d = elo(r.score), e = band(r, games);
        const base = ref.startsWith('sf') ? +ref.slice(2) : got[ref] ?? BOTS.byId(ref).rating, baseErr = ref.startsWith('sf') ? 0 : err[ref] ?? 0;
        got[id] = Math.round(base + d); err[id] = Math.round(Math.sqrt(e * e + baseErr * baseErr));
        console.log(`${line(a, b, r, games)}  → ${bot.name} measures ${got[id]} ±${err[id]} (target ${bot.rating})`);
      }
    }
  } finally { pool.stop(); console.log(`(${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)} s)`); }
})().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });
```

## tests/core.test.js

Tests of src/core.js; also checks these packs are up to date. (171 lines)

```js
// Tests of src/core.js (the chess rules), and a check that the context packs match the code and notes.
// Run with:  node tests/core.test.js   (no installs needed)
const assert = require('assert');
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path');
const C = require('../src/core.js');
let passed = 0, failed = 0;
function test(name, fn) {
  try { fn(); passed++; console.log('  ok   ' + name); }
  catch (e) { failed++; console.log('  FAIL ' + name + '\n       ' + e.message); }
}
// plays moves given as e2e4 and so on, failing on any that isn't legal
const moves = (list, g = C.newGame()) => { for (const s of list.split(' ')) { const n = C.playUci(g, s); assert.ok(n, `${s} should be legal after ${g.list.map(x => x.san).join(' ') || 'the start'}`); g = n; } return g; };
const sans = g => g.list.map(x => x.san).join(' ');
const legal = fen => C.legalMoves(C.parseFen(fen)).map(C.uci).sort();

console.log('\nPositions');
test('core.js runs in Node without a browser', () => assert.strictEqual(typeof C.legalMoves, 'function'));
test('the starting position has the 32 pieces in their places, White to move', () => {
  const p = C.parseFen(C.START);
  assert.strictEqual(p.b.filter(Boolean).length, 32);
  assert.strictEqual(p.b[C.sq('e1')], 'K'); assert.strictEqual(p.b[C.sq('d8')], 'q'); assert.strictEqual(p.b[C.sq('a1')], 'R');
  assert.strictEqual(p.turn, 'w'); assert.strictEqual(p.castle, 'KQkq');
});
test('FEN reads and writes back unchanged', () => {
  for (const f of [C.START, 'r3k2r/p1ppqpb1/bn2pnp1/3PN3/1p2P3/2N2Q1p/PPPBBPPP/R3K2R w KQkq - 0 1', '8/8/8/3pP3/8/8/8/4K2k w - d6 0 3'])
    assert.strictEqual(C.toFen(C.parseFen(f)), f);
});
test('a broken FEN is refused', () => {
  for (const f of ['', 'rnbqkbnr/pppppppp/8/8/8/8/PPPPPPPP w KQkq - 0 1', 'rnbqkbnr/pppppppp/9/8/8/8/PPPPPPPP/RNBQKBNR w KQkq - 0 1', '8/8/8/8/8/8/8/8 w - - 0 1', 'rnbqkbnr/pppppppp/8/8/8/8/PPPPPPPP/RNBQKBNR x KQkq - 0 1'])
    assert.throws(() => C.parseFen(f), `"${f}" was accepted`);
});

console.log('\nMove generation (perft: positions after n moves, against the published counts)');
const PERFT = [
  ['the starting position', C.START, [20, 400, 8902, 197281]],
  ['"Kiwipete": castling, pins, en passant', 'r3k2r/p1ppqpb1/bn2pnp1/3PN3/1p2P3/2N2Q1p/PPPBBPPP/R3K2R w KQkq - 0 1', [48, 2039, 97862]],
  ['an endgame with en passant and checks along the rank', '8/2p5/3p4/KP5r/1R3p1k/8/4P1P1/8 w - - 0 1', [14, 191, 2812, 43238]],
  ['promotions and castling out of check', 'r3k2r/Pppp1ppp/1b3nbN/nP6/BBP1P3/q4N2/Pp1P2PP/R2Q1RK1 w kq - 0 1', [6, 264, 9467]],
  ['a promotion with a capture, and a discovered check', 'rnbq1k1r/pp1Pbppp/2p5/8/2B5/8/PPP1NnPP/RNBQK2R w KQ - 1 8', [44, 1486, 62379]],
];
for (const [name, fen, counts] of PERFT) test(name, () => {
  const p = C.parseFen(fen);
  assert.deepStrictEqual(counts.map((_, i) => C.perft(p, i + 1)), counts);
});

console.log('\nSpecial moves');
test('castling both ways, and the rook moves with the king', () => {
  const g = moves('e2e4 e7e5 g1f3 b8c6 f1c4 g8f6 e1g1');
  assert.strictEqual(g.pos.b[C.sq('g1')], 'K'); assert.strictEqual(g.pos.b[C.sq('f1')], 'R'); assert.strictEqual(g.pos.b[C.sq('h1')], '');
  assert.strictEqual(g.pos.castle, 'kq');
  const q = moves('d2d4 d7d5 b1c3 b8c6 c1f4 c8f5 d1d2 d8d7 e1c1');
  assert.strictEqual(q.pos.b[C.sq('c1')], 'K'); assert.strictEqual(q.pos.b[C.sq('d1')], 'R'); assert.strictEqual(q.list[8].san, 'O-O-O');
});
test('no castling out of, through or into check, or after the king or rook has moved', () => {
  // the black bishop on c4 covers f1, so White can't castle king's side
  assert.ok(!legal('4k3/8/8/8/2b5/8/8/4K2R w K - 0 1').includes('e1g1'));
  assert.ok(!legal('4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/R3K2r w Q - 0 1').includes('e1c1'), 'castled out of check');
  assert.ok(legal('1r2k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/R3K3 w Q - 0 1').includes('e1c1'), 'a rook covering b1 stopped queen\'s side castling (the king never crosses b1)');
  const g = moves('e2e4 e7e5 e1e2 e8e7 e2e1 e7e8');
  assert.strictEqual(g.pos.castle, '');
  const r = moves('h2h4 a7a5 h1h3 a8a6');
  assert.strictEqual(r.pos.castle, 'Qk');
});
test('taking a rook on its home square takes away that castling right', () => {
  const g = moves('b2b3 g7g5 c1b2 g5g4 b2h8');
  assert.strictEqual(g.pos.castle, 'KQq');
});
test('en passant: only straight after the two-square move', () => {
  const g = moves('e2e4 a7a6 e4e5 d7d5');
  assert.strictEqual(C.toFen(g.pos).split(' ')[3], 'd6');
  const ep = moves('e5d6', g);
  assert.strictEqual(ep.pos.b[C.sq('d5')], ''); assert.strictEqual(ep.list[4].san, 'exd6');
  assert.ok(!C.playUci(moves('h2h3 h7h6', g), 'e5d6'), 'en passant was still allowed a move later');
});
test('en passant is refused when it would leave the king in check', () => {
  // after d7-d5, exd6 would open the fifth rank to the rook on h5
  assert.ok(!legal('8/8/8/K2pP2r/8/8/8/7k w - d6 0 1').includes('e5d6'));
});
test('promotion to any piece, and the move needs the choice', () => {
  const fen = '8/4P3/8/8/8/8/k7/4K3 w - - 0 1';
  assert.deepStrictEqual(legal(fen).filter(m => m.startsWith('e7e8')), ['e7e8b', 'e7e8n', 'e7e8q', 'e7e8r']);
  const g = C.newGame(fen);
  assert.strictEqual(C.play(g, { from: C.sq('e7'), to: C.sq('e8') }), null, 'a promotion was played without a choice');
  const n = C.play(g, { from: C.sq('e7'), to: C.sq('e8'), promo: 'n' });
  assert.strictEqual(n.pos.b[C.sq('e8')], 'N'); assert.strictEqual(n.list[0].san, 'e8=N');
  assert.strictEqual(C.playUci(C.newGame('4k3/8/8/8/8/8/3p4/6K1 b - - 0 1'), 'd2d1q').list[0].san, 'd1=Q+');
});
test('a pinned piece can\'t move off the line of the pin', () => {
  const ms = legal('4k3/4r3/8/8/8/8/4B3/4K3 w - - 0 1');
  assert.ok(!ms.some(m => m.startsWith('e2')), 'the pinned bishop moved');
});

console.log('\nEnd of the game');
test('checkmate: the fool\'s mate', () => {
  const g = moves('f2f3 e7e5 g2g4 d8h4');
  assert.strictEqual(sans(g), 'f3 e5 g4 Qh4#');
  assert.deepStrictEqual(g.result, { score: '0-1', why: 'checkmate' });
  assert.strictEqual(C.playUci(g, 'a2a3'), null, 'a move was played after the game ended');
});
test('stalemate', () => {
  const g = moves('b1b6', C.newGame('k7/8/8/2K5/8/8/8/1Q6 w - - 0 1'));
  assert.deepStrictEqual(g.result, { score: '1/2-1/2', why: 'stalemate' }, 'expected stalemate after Qb6');
});
test('a third repetition is a draw', () => {
  const g = moves('g1f3 g8f6 f3g1 f6g8 g1f3 g8f6 f3g1');
  assert.strictEqual(g.result, null, 'drawn too soon');
  assert.deepStrictEqual(moves('f6g8', g).result, { score: '1/2-1/2', why: 'repetition' });
});
test('fifty moves each without a capture or a pawn move is a draw', () => {
  const g = C.newGame('4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/R3K3 w - - 99 80');
  assert.deepStrictEqual(moves('a1a2', g).result, { score: '1/2-1/2', why: 'fifty' });
  assert.strictEqual(moves('e1d1', C.newGame('4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/R3K3 w - - 98 80')).result, null);
});
test('mate on the fiftieth move still counts as mate', () => {
  const g = moves('h1h8', C.newGame('4k3/R7/4K3/8/8/8/8/7R w - - 99 80'));
  assert.strictEqual(g.result.why, 'checkmate');
});
test('too few pieces to mate is a draw', () => {
  for (const f of ['4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/4K3 w - - 0 1', '4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/2B1K3 w - - 0 1', '4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/1N2K3 w - - 0 1', '2b1k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/4KB2 w - - 0 1'])
    assert.deepStrictEqual(C.newGame(f).result, { score: '1/2-1/2', why: 'material' }, f);
  for (const f of ['1b2k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/4KB2 w - - 0 1', '4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/1NN1K3 w - - 0 1', '4k3/8/8/8/8/8/P7/4K3 w - - 0 1'])
    assert.strictEqual(C.newGame(f).result, null, f + ' was called a draw');
  assert.strictEqual(moves('e1d1', C.newGame('4k3/8/8/8/8/8/8/3rK3 w - - 0 1')).result.why, 'material', 'taking the last piece didn\'t draw');
});
test('resigning and agreeing a draw', () => {
  const g = moves('e2e4');
  assert.deepStrictEqual(C.finish(g, '1-0', 'resigned').result, { score: '1-0', why: 'resigned' });
  assert.deepStrictEqual(C.finish(g, '1/2-1/2', 'agreed').result, { score: '1/2-1/2', why: 'agreed' });
});
test('taking a move back', () => {
  const g = moves('e2e4 e7e5 g1f3');
  const u = C.undo(g);
  assert.strictEqual(sans(u), 'e4 e5'); assert.strictEqual(u.pos.turn, 'w'); assert.strictEqual(u.keys.length, 3);
  assert.strictEqual(C.toFen(C.undo(C.undo(u)).pos), C.START);
  const m = C.undo(moves('f2f3 e7e5 g2g4 d8h4'));
  assert.strictEqual(m.result, null, 'the mate wasn\'t taken back');
});

console.log('\nNotation');
test('moves are written as in a score sheet', () => {
  assert.strictEqual(sans(moves('e2e4 d7d5 e4d5 d8d5 b1c3 d5a5 d2d4 g8f6 g1f3 c8f5 f1c4 e7e6 e1g1')), 'e4 d5 exd5 Qxd5 Nc3 Qa5 d4 Nf6 Nf3 Bf5 Bc4 e6 O-O');
});
test('two pieces that could reach the same square are told apart', () => {
  // knights on b1 and f3 can both go to d2: by file; rooks on a1 and a5 can both go to a3: by rank
  const n = C.newGame('4k3/8/8/8/8/5N2/8/1N2K3 w - - 0 1');
  assert.strictEqual(C.play(n, { from: C.sq('b1'), to: C.sq('d2') }).list[0].san, 'Nbd2');
  const r = C.newGame('4k3/8/8/R7/8/8/8/R3K3 w - - 0 1');
  assert.strictEqual(C.play(r, { from: C.sq('a5'), to: C.sq('a3') }).list[0].san, 'R5a3');
  // queens on d1, f1 and d3 can all reach e2: by square
  const q = C.newGame('7k/8/8/8/8/3Q4/8/3Q1QK1 w - - 0 1');
  assert.strictEqual(C.play(q, { from: C.sq('d1'), to: C.sq('e2') }).list[0].san, 'Qd1e2');
});
test('a game saved as e2e4-style moves plays back the same', () => {
  const g = moves('e2e4 c7c5 g1f3 d7d6 d2d4 c5d4 f3d4 g8f6 b1c3 a7a6');
  const saved = g.list.map(x => C.uci(x.m)).join(' ');
  assert.strictEqual(C.toFen(moves(saved).pos), C.toFen(g.pos));
});

console.log('\nNotes');
test('CONTEXT.md and CONTEXT-SHORT.md are up to date (if not, run node tools/build.js)', () => {
  const made = require('../tools/make-context.js').make();
  for (const [f, text] of Object.entries(made)) {
    const p = path.join(__dirname, '..', f);
    assert.ok(fs.existsSync(p), `${f} is missing`);
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8') === text, `${f} is out of date`);
  }
});

console.log(`\n${passed} passed, ${failed} failed`);
process.exit(failed ? 1 : 0);
```

## tests/browser/lib.js

Shared helpers for the browser tests. (48 lines)

```js
// Shared bits for the browser tests: Playwright, the addresses of the local test sites and relay, and where screenshots
// go. run-all.js builds the game and starts the sites and the relay; AGENTS.md (Testing) explains the set-up.
const path = require('path'), os = require('os'), fs = require('fs');

function loadPlaywright() {
  const tries = [process.env.PLAYWRIGHT_MODULE, 'playwright', '/opt/node-tools/node_modules/playwright'].filter(Boolean);
  for (const t of tries) { try { return require(t); } catch (e) {} }
  console.error('Playwright not found. Install it (npm install --no-save playwright), or set PLAYWRIGHT_MODULE to its folder.');
  process.exit(2);
}
const pw = loadPlaywright();

const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');                                  // the repository
const WORK = process.env.RS_WORK || path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'retro-set-browser-tests');  // test sites, relay copy, screenshots
const FILE = 'file://' + path.join(ROOT, 'index.html');                             // the built game, opened from disk
// Two copies of the game served like GitHub Pages (under /retro-set/): the current build, and the current build with
// its version changed (to stand in for "a different version"). Ports differ from Retro Rack's, so both projects' tests
// can run at once. RELAY: the local relay, for the online tests (?relay= in a page's address).
const SITE = { new: 'http://127.0.0.1:8180/retro-set/', alt: 'http://127.0.0.1:8182/retro-set/' };
const PORTS = { new: 8180, alt: 8182, app: 8190, relay: 8797 };
const RELAY = `?relay=ws://127.0.0.1:${PORTS.relay}`;
const shot = name => { const d = path.join(WORK, 'shots'); fs.mkdirSync(d, { recursive: true }); return path.join(d, name); };
// software WebGL, as in a Claude Code cloud session (no graphics card)
const GL = ['--use-gl=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'];

// The game's default picture (pixel size 1x, full colours) is slow to draw in software, so test pages start at 3x and
// 8 shades, unless they bring settings of their own or the address asks for the real defaults (?realdefaults).
function quick() {
  try { if (!/realdefaults/.test(location.search) && !localStorage.getItem('retroSet.settings')) localStorage.setItem('retroSet.settings', JSON.stringify({ pixel: 3, levels: 8 })); } catch (e) {}
}
const chromium = Object.create(pw.chromium);
chromium.launch = async (o = {}, ...more) => {
  const b = await pw.chromium.launch({ args: GL, ...o }, ...more), newContext = b.newContext.bind(b);
  b.newContext = async (...args) => { const c = await newContext(...args); await c.addInitScript(quick); return c; };
  b.newPage = async (...o) => { const c = await b.newContext(...o), p = await c.newPage(); p.on('close', () => c.close().catch(() => {})); return p; };
  return b;
};
// collects anything that reaches the browser console, so each test can check it stayed clean
function watch(p, log = []) {
  p.on('console', m => { if (!/GPU stall due to ReadPixels/.test(m.text())) log.push(`[${m.type()}] ${m.text()}`); });
  p.on('pageerror', e => log.push(`[pageerror] ${e.message}`));
  p.on('requestfailed', r => log.push(`[requestfailed] ${r.url()}`));
  return log;
}
const ok = (c, msg) => { console.log((c ? 'PASS ' : 'FAIL ') + msg); if (!c) process.exitCode = 1; };

module.exports = { ...pw, chromium, ROOT, WORK, FILE, SITE, PORTS, RELAY, shot, watch, ok };
```

## tests/browser/run-all.js

Runs the browser tests against a local copy of the site. (98 lines)

```js
// Runs the browser tests: builds the game, serves two copies of it locally like GitHub Pages (the current build, and
// the current build with a different version number), starts the relay locally, then runs each *.test.js in this
// folder in turn and prints a summary. Usage:
//   node tests/browser/run-all.js            every test
//   node tests/browser/run-all.js fit file   only tests whose file name contains one of the words
// Needs Playwright with Chromium, and npm for the relay's packages (installed once into a temporary folder).
const { spawn, execFileSync } = require('child_process');
const http = require('http'), fs = require('fs'), path = require('path');
const { ROOT, WORK, PORTS } = require('./lib');

const TYPES = { '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript', '.png': 'image/png', '.webmanifest': 'application/manifest+json', '.md': 'text/plain' };
const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
const say = t => console.log(`\x1b[36m${t}\x1b[0m`);

// a static server that behaves like GitHub Pages for this repository: everything under /retro-set/
function serve(dir, port) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const s = http.createServer((req, res) => {
      let p = decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url, 'http://x').pathname);
      if (!p.startsWith('/retro-set/')) { res.writeHead(404); return res.end('not found'); }
      p = p.slice('/retro-set'.length); if (p.endsWith('/')) p += 'index.html';
      const f = path.join(dir, p);
      if (!f.startsWith(dir) || !fs.existsSync(f) || fs.statSync(f).isDirectory()) { res.writeHead(404); return res.end('not found'); }
      res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': TYPES[path.extname(f)] || 'application/octet-stream', 'cache-control': 'max-age=600' });
      fs.createReadStream(f).pipe(res);
    });
    s.on('error', reject); s.listen(port, '127.0.0.1', () => resolve(s));
  });
}
function copySite(dir, html) {
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  for (const f of ['sw.js', 'manifest.webmanifest', 'README.md']) fs.copyFileSync(path.join(ROOT, f), path.join(dir, f));
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'config.js'), `window.RETRO_SET_RELAY = "ws://127.0.0.1:${PORTS.relay}";\n`);   // the local relay, never a real one
  fs.cpSync(path.join(ROOT, 'icons'), path.join(dir, 'icons'), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'index.html'), html);
}
const get = url => new Promise(r => { http.get(url, res => { let b = ''; res.on('data', d => b += d); res.on('end', () => r(b)); }).on('error', () => r(null)); });

// the relay, run locally by Wrangler (Cloudflare's own runtime), started fresh each run so no rooms are left over
async function startRelay() {
  if (/relay is running/.test(await get(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORTS.relay}/`) || '')) { say(`Using the relay already running on port ${PORTS.relay}.`); return null; }
  const dir = path.join(WORK, 'relay');
  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  for (const f of ['package.json', 'package-lock.json', 'wrangler.jsonc']) fs.copyFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'relay', f), path.join(dir, f));
  fs.cpSync(path.join(ROOT, 'relay', 'src'), path.join(dir, 'src'), { recursive: true });
  if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, 'node_modules', 'wrangler'))) {
    say('Installing the relay\'s packages (first run only)…');
    execFileSync('npm', ['ci', '--no-audit', '--no-fund'], { cwd: dir, stdio: 'inherit' });
  }
  fs.rmSync(path.join(dir, '.wrangler', 'state'), { recursive: true, force: true });
  say('Starting the relay locally…');
  const log = fs.openSync(path.join(WORK, 'relay.log'), 'w');
  const p = spawn('npx', ['wrangler', 'dev', '--port', String(PORTS.relay), '--ip', '127.0.0.1'], { cwd: dir, stdio: ['ignore', log, log], env: { ...process.env, WRANGLER_SEND_METRICS: 'false' }, detached: true });
  for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) { if (/relay is running/.test(await get(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORTS.relay}/`) || '')) return p; await sleep(1000); }
  throw new Error(`The relay didn't start; see ${path.join(WORK, 'relay.log')}`);
}

(async () => {
  const only = process.argv.slice(2);
  const tests = fs.readdirSync(__dirname).filter(f => f.endsWith('.test.js') && (!only.length || only.some(w => f.includes(w)))).sort();
  if (!tests.length) { console.error('No tests match.'); process.exit(2); }
  fs.mkdirSync(WORK, { recursive: true });

  say('Building index.html…');
  execFileSync(process.execPath, [path.join(ROOT, 'tools', 'build.js')], { stdio: 'inherit' });
  const html = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'index.html'), 'utf8');
  copySite(path.join(WORK, 'www-new'), html);
  copySite(path.join(WORK, 'www-alt'), html.replace(/const BUILD = '[0-9a-f]{8}'/, "const BUILD = 'deadbeef'"));

  const servers = [], relay = await startRelay();
  const stop = () => { for (const s of servers) s.close(); if (relay) try { process.kill(-relay.pid); } catch (e) {} };
  process.on('SIGINT', () => { stop(); process.exit(130); });
  try { servers.push(await serve(path.join(WORK, 'www-new'), PORTS.new), await serve(path.join(WORK, 'www-alt'), PORTS.alt)); }
  catch (e) { stop(); console.error(`Couldn't start the test sites (${e.message}). Is something else using ports ${PORTS.new} and ${PORTS.alt}?`); process.exit(2); }

  const results = [];
  for (const t of tests) {
    say(`\n=== ${t}`);
    const t0 = Date.now(); let pass = 0, fail = 0;
    const code = await new Promise(resolve => {
      const c = spawn(process.execPath, [path.join(__dirname, t)], { cwd: WORK, env: { ...process.env, RS_WORK: WORK } });
      let buf = '';
      const line = l => { if (/^PASS /.test(l)) pass++; if (/^FAIL /.test(l)) fail++; console.log(l); };
      c.stdout.on('data', d => { buf += d; const ls = buf.split('\n'); buf = ls.pop(); ls.forEach(line); });
      c.stderr.on('data', d => process.stderr.write(d));
      c.on('close', k => { if (buf) line(buf); resolve(k); });
    });
    results.push({ t, pass, fail, code, s: Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000) });
  }
  stop();

  say('\n=== Summary');
  for (const r of results) console.log(`${r.fail || r.code ? 'FAIL' : 'ok  '}  ${r.t.padEnd(28)} ${String(r.pass).padStart(3)} passed, ${r.fail} failed${r.code ? `, exit code ${r.code}` : ''}  (${r.s} s)`);
  const bad = results.filter(r => r.fail || r.code).length;
  console.log(bad ? `\n${bad} test file(s) failed.` : `\nAll ${results.reduce((a, r) => a + r.pass, 0)} checks passed. Screenshots: ${path.join(WORK, 'shots')}`);
  process.exit(bad ? 1 : 0);
})().catch(e => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });
```
